| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 67 | \| Straight-line AfA: completed after 31 Dec 2022 / 1925 to 2022 / before 1925 \| 3% / 2% / 2.5% a year \| [§ 7(4) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__7.html) \| | [39, 40, 41] / ['\| Straight-line AfA, building completed after 31 Dec 2022 \| 3% a year \| 2026 \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__7.html (§ 7(4) no. 2(a)) \|', '\| Straight-line AfA, completed 1 Jan 1925 to 31 Dec 2022 \| 2% a year \| 2026 \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__7.html (§ 7(4) no. 2(b)) \|', '\| Straight-line AfA, completed before 1 Jan 1925 \| 2.5% a year \| 2026 \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__7.html (§ 7(4) no. 2(c)) \|'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 68 | \| Declining-balance AfA (choice, housing) \| 5% of remaining value \| § 7(5a) EStG \| | [42, 88] / ['\| Declining-balance AfA for housing, construction start or purchase contract 1 Oct 2023 to 30 Sep 2029 \| 5% of remaining value \| 2026 \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__7.html (§ 7(5a)) \|', '- **Declining-balance AfA, § 7(5a).** Instead of 3% straight-line, 5% of the remaining value ("Restwert") each year, so far as the building is used for **housing**, if all hold: it is in Germany, the EU or the EEA; the client built it, or bought it by the end of the completion year; and construction started (the date on the construction-start notice required by state law; where none is required, the client declares a voluntary notice) or the binding purchase contract was made after 30 September 2023 and before 1 October 2029. No extraordinary write-down while it runs. Switching to straight-line is allowed, then on the remaining value and remaining life.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 69 | \| § 7b special depreciation, four years \| up to 5% a year \| [§ 7b EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__7b.html) \| | [43, 97] / ['\| § 7b special depreciation for new rental flats, year of purchase or completion and three more \| up to 5% a year of the base \| 2026 \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__7b.html \|', 'Up to 5% of the base in the year of acquisition or production and the three following years, on top of normal AfA under § 7(4) or § 7(5a). All must hold:'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 70 | \| § 7b cost cliff / base cap, new window \| EUR 5,200 / EUR 4,000 per m² \| § 7b(2), (3) EStG \| | [44, 100] / ['\| § 7b cost cliff / base cap, application 1 Jan 2023 to 30 Sep 2029 \| EUR 5,200 / EUR 4,000 per m² of living space \| 2026 \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__7b.html (§ 7b(2), (3)) \|', '- Cost per m² of living space **not more than** the cliff (EUR 3,000 old window, EUR 5,200 new window), or no § 7b at all. The base is capped per m² (EUR 2,000 old, EUR 4,000 new). Normal AfA still runs on full cost (BMF letter of 21 May 2025, para. 65).'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 71 | \| § 7b cost cliff / base cap, old window \| EUR 3,000 / EUR 2,000 per m² \| § 7b(2), (3) EStG \| | [45, 100, 104] / ['\| § 7b cost cliff / base cap, application 1 Sep 2018 to 31 Dec 2021 \| EUR 3,000 / EUR 2,000 per m²; last year of claim 2026 \| 2026 \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__52.html (§ 52(15a)) \|', '- Cost per m² of living space **not more than** the cliff (EUR 3,000 old window, EUR 5,200 new window), or no § 7b at all. The base is capped per m² (EUR 2,000 old, EUR 4,000 new). Normal AfA still runs on full cost (BMF letter of 21 May 2025, para. 65).', '- **Old window ends with 2026**: claims are allowed "letztmalig für den Veranlagungszeitraum 2026" ([§ 52(15a)](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__52.html)), even if four years have not run. A buyer who started in 2024 claims 2024 to 2026 and loses the fourth year (BMF letter, para. 16, example 1). The new window has no such end.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 72 | \| Works within three years capitalised if \| more than 15% of building cost, without VAT \| [§ 6(1) no. 1a EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__6.html) \| | [46, 119] / ["\| Works within three years of purchase capitalised if, without VAT, they exceed \| 15% of the building's acquisition cost \| 2026 \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__6.html (§ 6(1) no. 1a) \|", '- **The 15% rule, acquisition-related production cost** ([§ 6(1) no. 1a EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__6.html), applied to Werbungskosten by § 9(5) sentence 2). Repair and modernisation works carried out **within three years** of acquisition become production cost if their total cost **without VAT** "15 Prozent der Anschaffungskosten des Gebäudes übersteigen". It is a cliff: over it, **all** of them are capitalised. The three years are added up, so a later job can pull earlier deductions back in. Exactly 15% is not over. Not counted: extensions, and maintenance that usually recurs every year, such as servicing.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 73 | \| Low-value item deducted at once \| not more than EUR 800 net \| § 6(2) EStG \| | [48, 125] / ['\| Low-value movable item (furniture) deducted at once \| cost not more than EUR 800 net \| 2026 \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__6.html (§ 6(2)), via § 9(1) no. 7 \|', 'A furniture surcharge is rent. Furniture and fitted kitchens are depreciated over their useful life (§ 7(1) EStG; lines 42 to 45). An item usable on its own costing not more than EUR 800 net may be deducted at once ([§ 6(2) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__6.html) through § 9(1) no. 7). For the cheap-let comparison, use a furniture adjustment already provided by the local rent index without adding it twice. Otherwise add a surcharge only if it can be established from the local market for furnished lets; if no market value can be determined, no surcharge is added. Never derive it from monthly furniture AfA or a standard investment return ([BFH, 6 February 2018, IX R 14/17, paras. 18 to 20](https://www.bundesfinanzhof.de/de/entscheidung/entscheidungen-online/detail/pdf/STRE201810105?type=1646225765)).'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 74 | \| Cheap let: split / fully paid \| under 50% / at least 66% of market rent \| [§ 21(2) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__21.html) \| | [49, 50, 133, 135] / ['\| Cheap let of a home: costs split if rent is \| under 50% of local market rent \| 2026 \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__21.html (§ 21(2)) \|', '\| Cheap let of a permanent home: fully paid if rent is \| at least 66% of local rent \| 2026 \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__21.html (§ 21(2)) \|', '\| Less than 50% \| Split into a paid and an unpaid part; costs deductible only for the paid part \|', '\| At least 66%, permanent home let \| Deemed fully paid; all costs deductible \|'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 75 | \| Car travel to the property \| 30 cents per km driven \| [§ 5(2) BRKG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/brkg_2005/__5.html) \| | [51, 76] / ['\| Car travel to the property (actual cost or flat rate) \| 30 cents per km driven \| 2026 \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/brkg_2005/__5.html (§ 5(2) BRKG) \|', '- **Travel**: actual cost, or 30 cents per km **driven** by car (§ 9(1) no. 4a and § 9(3) EStG with [§ 5(2) BRKG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/brkg_2005/__5.html)). If the property were a first place of activity, the commuting allowance of EUR 0.38 per full km one way applies instead. When a let property is one is not settled on the pages read: ask the accountant.'] |

| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 77 | \| Loss carry-back \| EUR 1,000,000 (EUR 2,000,000 joint) \| [§ 10d EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__10d.html) \| | [55, 171] / ['\| Loss carry-back \| EUR 1,000,000 (EUR 2,000,000 joint) \| 2026 \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__10d.html (§ 10d(1)) \|', '- **Carry-back**: up to EUR 1,000,000 (EUR 2,000,000 joint) to the year before, then the second year before; the client may waive it only in full.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 78 | \| Loss carry-forward above EUR 1 million (EUR 2 million joint) \| 70% of the excess for 2024 to 2027; 60% from 2028 \| § 10d(2) EStG; BGBl. 2024 I Nr. 108, Art. 6 (https://www.recht.bund.de/bgbl/1/2024/108/VO.html) \| | [56, 172] / ['\| Loss carry-forward above EUR 1 million (EUR 2 million joint) of total income \| 70% of the excess for 2024 to 2027; 60% from 2028 \| 2026 \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__10d.html; https://www.recht.bund.de/bgbl/1/2024/108/regelungstext.pdf (Art. 6, in force 1 Jan 2028) \|', '- **Carry-forward**: no time limit; unlimited up to total income of EUR 1 million (EUR 2 million joint), then 70% of the excess for 2024 to 2027 and 60% from 2028. Article 6 of BGBl. 2024 I No. 108 changes "70 Prozent" back to "60 Prozent" from 1 January 2028; the consolidated text on gesetze-im-internet.de does not show that yet. Fixed by a separate notice (§ 10d(4)).'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 79 | \| Private sale exempt if total gains are \| less than EUR 1,000 \| [§ 23(3) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__23.html) \| | [54, 180] / ['\| Private sale gains tax-free if total for the year is \| less than EUR 1,000 \| 2026 \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__23.html (§ 23(3)) \|', '- **Exemption limit**: tax-free only if all private-sale gains in the year total **less than** EUR 1,000; at EUR 1,000 or more, all is taxed. Losses offset only private-sale gains of the same year, and carry back or forward only against such gains (§ 23(3) sentences 7 and 8).'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 80 | \| Employee must file if untaxed income is \| more than EUR 410 \| [§ 46(2) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__46.html) \| | [57, 194] / ['\| Employee must file if untaxed side income is more than \| EUR 410 \| 2026 \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__46.html (§ 46(2) no. 1) \|', '- **Who must file.** A landlord who is assessed anyway files Anlage V with the return. An employee whose wage tax was withheld must file if untaxed side income, such as rent, is **more than** EUR 410 ([§ 46(2) no. 1 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__46.html)); if assessed, side income of not more than EUR 410 in total is taken off again, phased out above (§ 46(3), (5)).'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 81 | \| VAT on an opted let \| 19% \| [§ 12 UStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__12.html) \| | [59, 166] / ['\| VAT on a let where the landlord opts / on short-term guest lets \| 19% / 7% \| 2026 \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__12.html (§ 12(1), (2) no. 11) \|', 'Letting land and buildings is VAT-exempt (§ 4 no. 12 sentence 1 letter a UStG), except short-term guest rooms, parking spaces, short-term campsites and operating fixtures. The landlord may opt to tax only on a let **to another business for its business** ([§ 9(1) UStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__9.html)), and for land only so far as the tenant uses it **only** for sales that allow input VAT deduction (§ 9(2)). Never to a private tenant. Some buildings begun before 1 June 1984 or 11 November 1993 escape that test ([§ 27(2) UStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__27.html)). If opted: VAT at 19% of net rent, input VAT recoverable; on the income tax return VAT received is income (line 27) and input VAT a cost (lines 80 to 82).'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 82 | \| Prepayments set only if \| at least EUR 400 a year and EUR 100 per date \| [§ 37(5) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__37.html) \| | [58, 197] / ['\| Prepayments set only if at least \| EUR 400 a year and EUR 100 per date \| 2026 \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__37.html (§ 37(5)) \|', "- **Prepayments** ([§ 37 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__37.html)): 10 March, 10 June, 10 September, 10 December; set only if at least EUR 400 a year and EUR 100 per date. A building's rental loss reduces prepayments only for calendar years beginning **after** its acquisition or completion (§ 37(3) sentence 8); a building bought before the year it is completed counts from completion (sentence 9); the bar does not apply where § 7b is claimed (sentence 10). The same timing governs a wage tax allowance for an employee ([§ 39a(1) no. 5 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__39a.html)). For example, a landlord who bought a new flat in 2026 with a EUR 10,050 loss cannot cut her 2026 prepayments for it; she can from 2027, or at once if § 7b is claimed."] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 83 | \| Late-filing surcharge per month \| 0.25%, at least EUR 25 \| [§ 152(5) AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__152.html) \| | [60, 220] / ['\| Late-filing surcharge per started month \| 0.25% of net tax due, at least EUR 25; cap EUR 25,000 \| 2026 \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__152.html (§ 152(5), (10)) \|', '- **Late-filing surcharge** ([§ 152 AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__152.html)). Compulsory if the return is not filed "binnen 14 Monaten nach Ablauf des Kalenderjahrs" (§ 152(2) no. 1), advised or not. For 2026 the 14 months end on 29 February 2028, the same day as the advised deadline, so for an advised return it bites from the day after that deadline. Also compulsory if a return the office called in early is not filed by the date set (no. 3). The 19-month period in no. 2 is only for farmers with a non-calendar financial year. Amount: 0.25% a month of the tax less prepayments and withheld tax, at least EUR 25 for each started month (§ 152(5)), rounded down to full euros and capped at EUR 25,000 (§ 152(10)). Not compulsory, though still possible at discretion, if the deadline was extended, the tax is nil or negative, or the tax does not exceed prepayments and withheld tax (§ 152(3)). For an unadvised 2025 return filed now the surcharge is discretionary until the 14 months run out at the end of February 2027.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 85 | ## The rules | [63] / ['## The taxable base'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 87 | ### Income and timing | [65] / ['### Income and timing'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 89 | - **Income**: rent, Umlagen, settlement back-payments, a deposit once kept and set off, a furniture surcharge, VAT received on an opted let. A refundable deposit is not income while held. | [67] / ['- **Income**: rent; Umlagen (running costs paid by the tenant); settlement back-payments; a deposit kept and set off against rent, running costs or damage; payments for antenna, wind or Wi-Fi sites; a furniture surcharge; VAT received on an opted let; grants for rent (2025 instructions, lines 13 to 31). A refundable deposit is not income while held.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 90 | - **Umlagen count twice**: income when received; the running costs paid are Werbungskosten. | [68] / ['- **Umlagen count twice**: income when received; the running costs paid are Werbungskosten.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 91 | - **Cash basis** (§ 11 EStG): income when received, costs when paid; regularly recurring items paid shortly around the turn of the year belong to their year. Rent received in advance for more than five years **may** be spread; costs paid in advance for more than five years **must** be spread. A market-level loan discount (Disagio) is deductible when paid. | [69] / ['- **Cash basis** ([§ 11 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__11.html)): income when received, costs when paid. Regularly recurring items paid shortly before or after the turn of the year belong to the year they relate to. Rent received in advance for a let of more than five years **may** be spread over the period; costs paid in advance for more than five years **must** be spread. A market-level loan discount (Disagio) is deductible when paid.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 93 | ### Building depreciation (AfA, [§ 7 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__7.html)) | [82] / ['### Building depreciation (AfA, [§ 7 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__7.html))'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 95 | - **Completion date decides the rate**, not the purchase date. The rates apply to every privately held building, housing or not, including condominiums and separate parts of a building (§ 7(5b)). The separate 3% rate for **business-asset** buildings not used for housing (§ 7(4) sentence 1 no. 1) is outside this skill; never use it for a private shop or office completed before 2023. | [84] / ['- **Completion date decides the straight-line rate**, not the purchase date: 3% after 2022, 2% for 1925 to 2022, 2.5% before 1925 (§ 7(4) sentence 1 no. 2). This covers every privately held building, housing or not, including flats and separate parts of a building (§ 7(5b)). The separate 3% rate in no. 1 is for business-asset buildings not used for housing: never use it for a private shop or office completed before 2023.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 96 | - **Shorter actual life.** If the actual useful life is under 33 years (built after 2022), 50 years (1925 to 2022) or 40 years (before 1925), that life may be used (§ 7(4) sentence 2). The 2025 instructions call this an exceptional case needing proof and cite the BMF letter of 22 February 2023. Refer. | [85, 344] / ['- **Shorter actual life.** If the actual life is under 33 years (after 2022), 50 years (1925 to 2022) or 40 years (before 1925), that life may be used (§ 7(4) sentence 2). The 2025 instructions call this an exceptional case that must be proved to the tax office. Refer.', 'Not yet confirmed: the 2026 Anlage V form and instructions (the recorded route returned no substantive content; line numbers are from 2025); any BMF update to the letter of 8 October 2004 after the retained 2024 handbook (the checked 2025 handbook route remained inaccessible); the BMF letter of 22 February 2023 on a shorter actual useful life; whether a let property can be a first place of activity for travel; AfA in the month of sale; heirs under § 23(1) sentence 3; treaty treatment of foreign rental property; the BMF letter of 21 October 2026 on paid waiver of usufruct (Nießbrauch), not read.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 97 | - **Older staged rates.** A building built or bought in its completion year under an application or contract before 1 January 2006 (housing; earlier dates otherwise) may be on the staged rates of § 7(5). Ask what past returns used; refer any change of method. | [86] / ['- **Older staged rates.** A building built or bought in its completion year under an application or contract before 1 January 2006 (housing; earlier dates for the other historic branches) may be on the staged rates of § 7(5). Ask what past returns used; refer any change.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 98 | - **Acquired for free**: continue the previous owner's rate (2025 instructions). | [87] / ["- **Acquired free of charge** (gift or inheritance): continue the previous owner's rate (2025 instructions)."] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 99 | - **Declining-balance, § 7(5a).** 5% of the remaining value, so far as the building is used for **housing**, if: it is in Germany, the EU or the EEA; the client built it or bought it by the end of the completion year; and construction started (per the construction-start notice required by state law, not the application date; where none is required, the client declares a voluntary report), or the binding purchase contract was made, after 30 September 2023 and before 1 October 2029. No extraordinary write-down while it runs. Switching to straight-line is allowed, then on remaining value and remaining life. | [88] / ['- **Declining-balance AfA, § 7(5a).** Instead of 3% straight-line, 5% of the remaining value ("Restwert") each year, so far as the building is used for **housing**, if all hold: it is in Germany, the EU or the EEA; the client built it, or bought it by the end of the completion year; and construction started (the date on the construction-start notice required by state law; where none is required, the client declares a voluntary notice) or the binding purchase contract was made after 30 September 2023 and before 1 October 2029. No extraordinary write-down while it runs. Switching to straight-line is allowed, then on the remaining value and remaining life.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 100 | - **Base.** Price plus side costs (transfer tax, notary, land register, the buyer's broker) under [§ 255 HGB](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/hgb/__255.html), less the land share. A maintenance reserve bought with a flat is excluded; grants reduce the base (line 89). A former own home let for the first time needs a first computation. | [89] / ["- **Base.** Price plus side costs (transfer tax, notary, land register, the buyer's broker) under [§ 255 HGB](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/hgb/__255.html), less the land share. Land is never depreciated. A maintenance reserve bought with a flat is excluded (2025 instructions). Grants reduce the base (line 89). A former own home let for the first time needs a first computation."] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 101 | - **Price split.** By the ratio of market values, "nicht nach der sogenannten Restwertmethode". The [BMF price-split tool](https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/Steuern/Berechnung-Aufteilung-Grundstueckskaufpreis.html) (March 2026) makes or tests a split. A contract split at arm's length or an expert valuation may also be used; the tax office may challenge a high building share. | [90] / ["- **Price split.** Split by the ratio of market values, not by the residual-value method (2025 instructions). The [BMF purchase-price split tool](https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/Steuern/Berechnung-Aufteilung-Grundstueckskaufpreis.html) makes or tests a split. A contract split at arm's length or an expert valuation may also be used; the tax office may challenge a high building share."] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 102 | - **First-year month cut.** Cut one twelfth for each full month before the month of acquisition or completion (§ 7(1) sentence 4), applied to § 7(5a) by statute and to straight-line by the finance ministry ("bei Fertigstellung nach dem 31. Januar wäre eine zeitanteilige Kürzung der linearen AfA im Jahr der Fertigstellung erforderlich", BMF letter of 21 May 2025, example 7). Only the old staged rates of § 7(5) take a full first year. | [91] / ['- **First-year month cut.** Cut one twelfth for each full month before the month of acquisition or completion (§ 7(1) sentence 4). Januar wäre eine zeitanteilige Kürzung der linearen AfA im Jahr der Fertigstellung erforderlich" (example 7). Only the old staged § 7(5) rates take a full first year.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 103 | - **End**: until fully written off or sold. | [93] / ['- **End**: until fully written off, or the year of sale.'] |

| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 117 | - **Old window ends with 2026**: claims "letztmalig für den Veranlagungszeitraum 2026" ([§ 52(15a) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__52.html)), even if four years have not run. | pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 118 | - **Claw-back** for the past if the flat is not let for housing for ten years, is sold in that time with the gain untaxed, or later costs push it over the cliff within three years after the year of acquisition or production (§ 7b(4)). | [105] / ['- **Claw-back** of all past claims if the flat is not let for housing for ten years, is sold in that time with the gain untaxed, or later costs push it over the cliff within three years after the year of acquisition or production (§ 7b(4)).'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 119 | - **After four years**, normal AfA runs on the remaining value ([§ 7a(9) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__7a.html); BMF letter, paragraph 66). Refer the computation. | [106] / ['- **After the four-year benefit period**, regular AfA uses the remaining value and the § 7(4) percentage determined from the remaining mathematical useful life; for § 7(5a), apply its 5% rate to that remaining value ([§ 7a(9) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__7a.html); BMF letter, para. 66). The old-window 2026 sunset does not shorten that four-year period: the 2024 buyer above still uses unchanged regular AfA in 2027 and changes to § 7a(9) in 2028 (BMF para. 16). Establish the actual historical normal/special deductions and useful-life branch before computing; do not substitute a guessed partial-year balance.'] |

| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 126 | Costs to get, secure and keep the income (§ 9(1) EStG), deductible in the year paid, only for a flat that earns or is intended to earn rent. Letting individual rooms within the owner’s home can generate deductible rental costs; allocate those costs to the actual let part.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 128 | - **Deductible**: loan interest so far as linked to the property, and costs of raising the loan; AfA and § 7b; property tax, public charges and building insurance (§ 9(1) sentence 3 no. 2); liability insurance; running costs passed on; Hausgeld **except** the maintenance-reserve part, which is deductible only when the community spends it; management; the tax adviser's rental share; tenancy legal fees; tenant advertising and broker; rent-account fees; maintenance. | [75] / ["- **Deductible**: loan interest so far as linked to the property, and the cost of raising the loan (§ 9(1) no. 1); property tax, other public charges and building insurance (no. 2); AfA and § 7b (no. 7); liability insurance; running costs, including those passed on; Hausgeld **except** the maintenance-reserve part, which is deductible only when the owners' association spends it; management; the tax adviser's rental share; tenancy legal fees; advertising and the broker for finding a tenant; rent-account fees; maintenance."] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 129 | - **Travel**: actual cost or 30 cents per km **driven** (§ 5(2) BRKG through § 9(1) sentence 3 no. 4a and § 9(3) EStG). If the property were a first place of activity, the commuting allowance of EUR 0.38 per full km one way applies instead. When a let property is one is not settled on the pages read: ask the accountant.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 130 | - **Not deductible**: principal (Tilgung); transfer tax on the purchase (acquisition cost); reserve payments until spent; own-use or free flats; the unpaid part of a cheap let; private costs; fines (§ 9(5) with § 4(5) no. 8 EStG); income tax, solidarity surcharge, church tax. | [77, 78, 79] / ['- **Not deductible**: loan principal (Tilgung); transfer tax, notary and broker on the purchase (they go into acquisition cost); reserve payments until spent; costs attributable to own-use or free-use parts; the unpaid part of a cheap let; fines (§ 9(5) with § 4(5) no. 8 EStG); income tax, solidarity surcharge and church tax.', '', 'For mixed private and rental use, first attribute identifiable costs directly. Allocate shared costs by a justified, documented measure, such as the actual usable-floor-area ratio, and explain a first allocation (published 2025 ELSTER instructions, applied here to the unchanged rental-expense principle, not claimed 2026 form lines). For example, an identifiable rented-room repair of EUR 1,000 plus a shared roof repair of EUR 10,000 at an independently supported 20% rental share gives EUR 3,000 rental costs before any other applicable limitation; private-only costs stay excluded. The 20% is a stipulated allocation fact, not a universal prescribed ratio.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 132 | ### Maintenance or production cost ([§ 6(1) no. 1a EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__6.html)) | [114] / ['### Maintenance or production cost'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 134 | - **Maintenance** (Erhaltungsaufwand) keeps or restores the condition: repainting, like-for-like heating, roof repair, equivalent windows. Deductible when paid, or spread under § 82b EStDV.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 135 | - **Production cost** creates something new, extends, or substantially improves beyond the original condition ([§ 255(2) HGB](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/hgb/__255.html)): balcony, attic conversion, lift, lifting the standard (judged mainly by heating, sanitary, electrics and windows). Capitalised. | [117] / ['- **Production cost** creates something new, extends, or substantially improves beyond the original condition ([§ 255(2) HGB](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/hgb/__255.html)): balcony, attic conversion, lift, lifting the standard (judged mainly by heating, sanitary and electrical installations and windows). The 2025 instructions: works that bring the flat or building "auf einen höheren Standard" are production or acquisition cost. Capitalised and depreciated with the building.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 136 | - **Bought in a poor state**: making non-functioning essential parts work again (defective heating, water or fire damage), and change-of-use works before first use, are **acquisition** cost (2025 instructions). | [118] / ['- **Bought in a poor state**: making non-working essential parts work again (defective heating, water or fire damage), and change-of-use works before first use, are **acquisition** cost (2025 instructions).'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 137 | - **15% rule** (§ 6(1) no. 1a EStG, applied by § 9(5) sentence 2). Repair and modernisation works carried out **within three years** of acquisition become production cost if their total cost **without VAT** is **more than** 15% of the **building's** acquisition cost ("ohne die Umsatzsteuer 15 Prozent der Anschaffungskosten des Gebäudes übersteigen"). A cliff: over it, **all** are capitalised. The three years are added up, so a later job can pull earlier deductions in. Exactly 15% is not over. Not counted: extensions, and maintenance that usually recurs yearly (servicing). | [119] / ['- **The 15% rule, acquisition-related production cost** ([§ 6(1) no. 1a EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__6.html), applied to Werbungskosten by § 9(5) sentence 2). Repair and modernisation works carried out **within three years** of acquisition become production cost if their total cost **without VAT** "15 Prozent der Anschaffungskosten des Gebäudes übersteigen". It is a cliff: over it, **all** of them are capitalised. The three years are added up, so a later job can pull earlier deductions back in. Exactly 15% is not over. Not counted: extensions, and maintenance that usually recurs every year, such as servicing.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 138 | - **Spreading, § 82b EStDV.** Larger maintenance may be spread evenly over two to five years if, **when incurred**, the building is not a business asset and is **mainly** housing: housing floor area more than half the usable area; garages count as housing up to one car per flat. On sale, move into a business or end of letting, the rest is deducted that year. Co-owners use one period. Works in redevelopment areas and on listed buildings (§§ 11a, 11b EStG): refer. | [121] / ['- **Spreading, [§ 82b EStDV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estdv_1955/__82b.html).** Larger maintenance may be spread evenly over two to five years if, **when paid**, the building is not a business asset and is **mainly** housing: housing floor area more than half the usable area; garages count as housing up to one car per flat. On sale, transfer into a business or end of letting, the rest is deducted that year. Co-owners must use the same period. Works in redevelopment areas and on listed buildings (§§ 11a, 11b EStG, §§ 7h, 7i): refer.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 140 | ### Furniture | [123] / ['### Furniture'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 142 | A furniture surcharge is rent. Furniture and fitted kitchens are depreciated over their useful life (§ 7(1) EStG; lines 42 to 45). An item usable on its own costing not more than EUR 800 net may be deducted at once ([§ 6(2) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__6.html)). How a furniture surcharge enters the cheap-let comparison is not on an official page read: refer. | [125, 344] / ['A furniture surcharge is rent. Furniture and fitted kitchens are depreciated over their useful life (§ 7(1) EStG; lines 42 to 45). An item usable on its own costing not more than EUR 800 net may be deducted at once ([§ 6(2) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__6.html) through § 9(1) no. 7). For the cheap-let comparison, use a furniture adjustment already provided by the local rent index without adding it twice. Otherwise add a surcharge only if it can be established from the local market for furnished lets; if no market value can be determined, no surcharge is added. Never derive it from monthly furniture AfA or a standard investment return ([BFH, 6 February 2018, IX R 14/17, paras. 18 to 20](https://www.bundesfinanzhof.de/de/entscheidung/entscheidungen-online/detail/pdf/STRE201810105?type=1646225765)).', 'Not yet confirmed: the 2026 Anlage V form and instructions (the recorded route returned no substantive content; line numbers are from 2025); any BMF update to the letter of 8 October 2004 after the retained 2024 handbook (the checked 2025 handbook route remained inaccessible); the BMF letter of 22 February 2023 on a shorter actual useful life; whether a let property can be a first place of activity for travel; AfA in the month of sale; heirs under § 23(1) sentence 3; treaty treatment of foreign rental property; the BMF letter of 21 October 2026 on paid waiver of usufruct (Nießbrauch), not read.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 144 | ### Cheap lets: the 50% and 66% rules ([§ 21(2) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__21.html)) | [127] / ['### Cheap lets: the 50% and 66% rules ([§ 21(2) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__21.html))'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 146 | Applies to letting a **home for living in**, to any tenant; it mostly bites on relatives. | [129] / ['Applies to letting a **home for living in**, to any tenant; it mostly bites on relatives.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 148 | \| Rent as share of local market rent \| Result \| | [127] / ['### Cheap lets: the 50% and 66% rules ([§ 21(2) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__21.html))'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 149 | \| --- \| --- \| | [127] / ['### Cheap lets: the 50% and 66% rules ([§ 21(2) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__21.html))'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 150 | \| Less than 50% ("weniger als 50 Prozent") \| Split into paid and unpaid parts; costs deductible only for the paid part \| | [133] / ['\| Less than 50% \| Split into a paid and an unpaid part; costs deductible only for the paid part \|'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 151 | \| At least 50% and less than 66% \| No split **only if** no loss is expected over the let (surplus forecast); 50% alone is not enough (2025 instructions: "mindestens 50 % , jedoch weniger als 66 %") \| | [134, 139, 145] / ['\| At least 50% and less than 66% \| Under the published 2025 ELSTER instruction, a forecast with no loss, including exactly zero, gives full cost deduction; a forecast loss requires splitting. This is the dated administrative method used here on the unchanged 2026 statutory band; the older BMF para. 13 uses positive-surplus wording. \|', '- **The forecast (Totalüberschussprognose)** for the 50% to 66% band, per the BMF letter of 8 October 2004 (BStBl I p. 933), paras. 13 and 34, as printed with its notes in the [official income tax handbook 2024, Anhang 30 II](https://esth.bundesfinanzministerium.de/esth/2024/C-Anhaenge/Anhang-30/II/anhang-30-II.html). The notes state that from 2021 para. 13 applies with 50% and 66% in place of 56% and 75%.', '  - **Result**: apply the published 2025 ELSTER no-loss instruction to this unchanged 2026 band: a zero or positive forecast gives full deduction; a negative forecast splits the let as under 50%. This identifies the current published administrative branch, rather than claiming that the older BMF positive-surplus wording expressly settles equality.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 152 | \| At least 66%, permanent home let ("mindestens 66 Prozent") \| Fully paid; all costs deductible \| | [135] / ['\| At least 66%, permanent home let \| Deemed fully paid; all costs deductible \|'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 154 | - **Compare** rent paid **including** Umlagen with market rent **including** passable running costs. Not cold rent with cold rent. | [137] / ['- **Compare** rent paid including Umlagen with market rent including passable running costs, never cold rent with cold rent (2025 instructions).'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 155 | - **Split**: costs in the ratio of agreed to market rent. On the 2025 form enter all costs in full (lines 33 to 84) and the cut percentage in line 87, or an amount in line 88 if only part is let cheaply. The tax office makes the cut. | [138] / ['- **The split**: costs in the ratio of agreed rent to market rent. On the 2025 form enter all costs in full (lines 33 to 84) and the cut percentage in line 87, or an amount in line 88 if only part of the property is let cheaply. The tax office makes the cut.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 156 | - **Forecast**: per the BMF letter of 8 October 2004 (paragraph 33 onward), not found on an allowed page. Refer. | [139, 140, 141, 142, 143, 144, 145] / ['- **The forecast (Totalüberschussprognose)** for the 50% to 66% band, per the BMF letter of 8 October 2004 (BStBl I p. 933), paras. 13 and 34, as printed with its notes in the [official income tax handbook 2024, Anhang 30 II](https://esth.bundesfinanzministerium.de/esth/2024/C-Anhaenge/Anhang-30/II/anhang-30-II.html). The notes state that from 2021 para. 13 applies with 50% and 66% in place of 56% and 75%.', '  - **Period**: 30 years, counted for the cheap let from the date of its tenancy contract (para. 34, example 2). Earlier lets of the same flat and other flats are left out.', '  - **AfA in the forecast**: the straight-line § 7(4) rate, not § 7(5a) declining balance or § 7b special depreciation actually claimed (para. 34).', '  - **Estimate**: average income and costs of the last five years, unless the client shows objective changes already set in motion; future maintenance may be estimated along § 28 of the Second Computation Ordinance (II. BV).', '  - **Safety margins**: add 10% to total estimated income and deduct 10% from total estimated costs.', '  - **Leave out** private sale gains, taxable under § 23 or not.', '  - **Result**: apply the published 2025 ELSTER no-loss instruction to this unchanged 2026 band: a zero or positive forecast gives full deduction; a negative forecast splits the let as under 50%. This identifies the current published administrative branch, rather than claiming that the older BMF positive-surplus wording expressly settles equality.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 157 | - **Market rent**: the local rent index (Mietspiegel); failing that, as equal-ranking examples, a sworn expert's report, a rent database, or three comparable flats (§ 558a(2) nos. 2 to 4 BGB). | [146] / ["- **Market rent**: the local rent index (Mietspiegel); failing that, equally valid: a sworn expert's reasoned report, a rent database, or rents for at least three comparable flats (§ 558a(2) nos. 2 to 4 BGB, per the 2025 instructions)."] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 158 | - **Outside**: commercial space; the 66% sentence covers only permanent home lets. | [147] / ['- **Outside § 21(2)**: commercial space; the 66% sentence covers only permanent home lets. A cheap let of an office to a relative: refer.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 160 | ### Vacancy, holiday flats and short-term lets | [149] / ['### Vacancy, holiday flats and the intention to make a surplus (Liebhaberei)'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 162 | - **Vacancy.** Costs stay deductible while the client intends to let. Keep evidence (listings, agent mandate, realistic rent); a long unmarketed vacancy risks denial. | [155] / ['- **Vacancy.** Costs are deductible before the first let and during a gap once the decision to let for good is made and shown by serious, continuing efforts (agent, advertisements, a realistic rent) (paras. 25 to 27). The client carries the proof. Without such efforts, or if the flat cannot be let in the foreseeable future, the intention lapses and costs stop (notes to para. 26).'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 163 | - **Form.** A holiday flat or house, or any object let **short-term** (for example through an internet platform), needs **Anlage V-FeWo** as well as Anlage V; up to 4 flats per form. | [156, 193] / ['- **Forms.** A holiday flat, or any object let **short-term** (for example through an online platform), needs **Anlage V-FeWo** as well as Anlage V (2025 instructions). Each V-FeWo form covers up to four flats.', '- **Forms.** One Anlage V per built-on property, filed with the income tax return through ELSTER. Add Anlage V-FeWo for holiday or short-term lets, and Anlage V-Sonstige for shares in communities and funds, subletting, land without buildings and rights (2025 instructions).'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 164 | - **Day counts** where the owner used it, left it free to others, or could use it at any time: days of own use (including free use by others; not short stays for servicing, cleaning, renovation or checks), letting and vacancy. Own use possible at any time: split vacancy by the ratio of own use to letting. Own use limited to set times (for example via an agent): only reserved time is own use; vacancy counts as letting. Never used by the owner: give the letting days usual in that place. | [157] / ['- **Day counts** where the owner used it, left it free to others, or could use it at any time: days of own use (including free use by others; not short stays for servicing, cleaning, renovation or checks), letting and vacancy. Own use possible at any time: split vacancy by the ratio of own use to letting. Own use limited to set times (for example let through an agent): only the reserved time is own use, and vacancy counts as letting. Never used by the owner and let only to changing guests: give the letting days usual in that place, from tourist office or municipal records.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 165 | - **Intention to make a surplus**: the instructions cite the BMF letter of 8 October 2004, not read on an allowed page. Refer holiday homes with own use. | [151, 158, 159, 160, 161] / ['Source for this part: BMF letter of 8 October 2004 (BStBl I p. 933), as printed with its notes in the [official income tax handbook 2024, Anhang 30 II](https://esth.bundesfinanzministerium.de/esth/2024/C-Anhaenge/Anhang-30/II/anhang-30-II.html) ("para." below).', '- **Holiday flat let only to changing guests** and otherwise held ready for them: the intention is taken as given without a test, whether the client lets it directly or through an agent (para. 16). Exception: if letting days fall short of the usual local letting time by **at least 25%**, with no obstacle to letting, a forecast is needed (note to para. 16).', "- **Proving there is no own use** (para. 17): the client shows one of: letting handed to an unrelated agent with own use excluded by contract for the whole year; the flat is in or right next to the client's own home, which meets the client's needs; the client has more than one holiday flat in the place and uses only one; or letting days at least equal the local seasonal average. Otherwise the client must prove it, and with too few letting days show stronger advertising (para. 18). Short stays for servicing, key handover, cleaning, checks, repairs or owners' meetings are not own use; longer stays or stays with family need explaining (para. 19).", '- **Holiday flat let part of the time and used by the owner** (or reserved for own use, or lent free): the intention is always tested by forecast (para. 21), per flat, not per let (para. 34). Only costs for let days and vacancy allocated to letting go in (para. 39); mixed costs (interest, property charges, maintenance, AfA, insurance) are split by days of each use (para. 40). Vacancy: if own use is limited to set times, only those count as own use; if possible at any time, split vacancy by the ratio of own use to letting; if the extent of own use cannot be established, vacancy is split 50/50 (paras. 22, 23). The forecast then follows para. 34: 30 years, § 7(4) AfA, five-year average, 10% margins (see the cheap-let forecast above).', '- **Without the intention** the losses are not recognised (Liebhaberei). If the lack of intention only shows later, final assessments can be changed under § 173(1) no. 1 AO (para. 10).'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 166 | - **Trade and VAT.** Hotel-like services may make it a trade (`de-trade-tax`). Short-term guest accommodation is not VAT-exempt ([§ 4 no. 12 UStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__4.html)); where VAT is due it takes the reduced rate of seven per cent ([§ 12(2) no. 11 UStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__12.html)). The reduced rate does not cover services not directly part of the letting, such as breakfast, even if included in the price (§ 12(2) no. 11 sentence 2). Whether it is due depends on the small-business rule: `germany-vat-return`. No § 7b for short-term lets. | [162] / ['- **Trade and VAT.** Hotel-like services may make it a trade (`de-trade-tax`). Short-term guest accommodation is not VAT-exempt ([§ 4 no. 12 sentence 2 UStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__4.html)); where VAT is due it is at 7% ([§ 12(2) no. 11 UStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__12.html)), except for services not directly part of the letting, such as breakfast, even if included in the price. Whether VAT is due turns on the small-business rule: `germany-vat-return`. No § 7b for short-term lets.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 168 | ### VAT option on commercial lets | [164] / ['### VAT option on commercial lets'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 170 | Letting is VAT-exempt (§ 4 no. 12 sentence 1 letter a UStG), except short-term accommodation, parking spaces, short-term campsites and operating fixtures. The landlord may opt only on a let **to a business for its business** ([§ 9 UStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__9.html)), and for land only so far as the tenant uses it **only** for sales that allow input VAT deduction, which the landlord must prove. Not to a private tenant. Some buildings started before 1 June 1984 or 11 November 1993 escape that test ([§ 27(2) UStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__27.html)). If opted: VAT at 19% of net rent, input VAT recoverable; on the income tax return VAT received is income (line 27) and input VAT a cost (lines 80 to 82). | [166] / ['Letting land and buildings is VAT-exempt (§ 4 no. 12 sentence 1 letter a UStG), except short-term guest rooms, parking spaces, short-term campsites and operating fixtures. The landlord may opt to tax only on a let **to another business for its business** ([§ 9(1) UStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__9.html)), and for land only so far as the tenant uses it **only** for sales that allow input VAT deduction (§ 9(2)). Never to a private tenant. Some buildings begun before 1 June 1984 or 11 November 1993 escape that test ([§ 27(2) UStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__27.html)). If opted: VAT at 19% of net rent, input VAT recoverable; on the income tax return VAT received is income (line 27) and input VAT a cost (lines 80 to 82).'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 172 | ### Losses ([§ 10d EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__10d.html)) | [168] / ['### Losses ([§ 10d EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__10d.html))'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 174 | - **Same year**: against other rental income, then other kinds of income (§ 2(3) EStG). | [170] / ['- **Same year**: against other rental income, then other kinds of income (§ 2(3) EStG).'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 175 | - **Carry-back**: up to EUR 1,000,000 (EUR 2,000,000 joint), to the year before and then the second year before; waivable only in full ("insgesamt abzusehen"). | [171] / ['- **Carry-back**: up to EUR 1,000,000 (EUR 2,000,000 joint) to the year before, then the second year before; the client may waive it only in full.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 176 | - **Carry-forward**: no time limit; unlimited up to total income of EUR 1 million (EUR 2 million joint), then 70% of the excess for 2024 to 2027 (§ 52(18b)) and 60% again from 2028: BGBl. 2024 I Nr. 108, Art. 6 (https://www.recht.bund.de/bgbl/1/2024/108/VO.html) changes "70 Prozent" back to "60 Prozent" from 1 January 2028, a change the consolidated text on gesetze-im-internet.de does not show yet. Fixed by a separate notice (§ 10d(4)). | [172] / ['- **Carry-forward**: no time limit; unlimited up to total income of EUR 1 million (EUR 2 million joint), then 70% of the excess for 2024 to 2027 and 60% from 2028. Article 6 of BGBl. 2024 I No. 108 changes "70 Prozent" back to "60 Prozent" from 1 January 2028; the consolidated text on gesetze-im-internet.de does not show that yet. Fixed by a separate notice (§ 10d(4)).'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 177 | - **Schemes**: §§ 15a, 15b apply by analogy (§ 21(1) sentence 2). Refer. | [173] / ['- **Loss schemes**: §§ 15a and 15b apply by analogy (§ 21(1) sentence 2). Refer.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 178 | - **Lasting losses** with no surplus in sight may be denied; no allowed page sets the test. A permanent home let at 66% or more is fully paid by statute. Refer doubtful cases. | [153, 154, 161] / ['- **Long-term home lets.** A let of a flat meant to last is taken to aim at a surplus without a test (para. 1). That presumption does not cover commercial premises or undeveloped land (note to para. 1): there the forecast below decides.', '- **Signs against the intention** (paras. 5 to 7): a let planned only for a time (a fixed-term lease with stated own use or sale, short financing, an early search for a buyer), or a sale or own use within about five years of acquisition with only losses in that time. The client can rebut by showing the decision to sell or move in came later (para. 9).', '- **Without the intention** the losses are not recognised (Liebhaberei). If the lack of intention only shows later, final assessments can be changed under § 173(1) no. 1 AO (para. 10).'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 179 | - **Prepayments**: a building's rental loss reduces prepayments only for calendar years beginning **after** its acquisition or completion ([§ 37(3) sentence 8 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__37.html)); if the building is bought before the year it is completed, count from completion (sentence 9). The bar does not apply where § 7b is claimed (sentence 10). The same timing governs a wage tax allowance ([§ 39a(1) no. 5 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__39a.html)). | [197] / ["- **Prepayments** ([§ 37 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__37.html)): 10 March, 10 June, 10 September, 10 December; set only if at least EUR 400 a year and EUR 100 per date. A building's rental loss reduces prepayments only for calendar years beginning **after** its acquisition or completion (§ 37(3) sentence 8); a building bought before the year it is completed counts from completion (sentence 9); the bar does not apply where § 7b is claimed (sentence 10). The same timing governs a wage tax allowance for an employee ([§ 39a(1) no. 5 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__39a.html)). For example, a landlord who bought a new flat in 2026 with a EUR 10,050 loss cannot cut her 2026 prepayments for it; she can from 2027, or at once if § 7b is claimed."] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 181 | ### Sale within ten years ([§ 23 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__23.html)) | [175] / ['### Sale within ten years ([§ 23 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__23.html), the Spekulationsfrist)'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 183 | - A private sale if acquisition and sale are **not more than** ten years apart; buildings put up or extended in the period are included. | [177] / ['- A sale of land or a flat is a private sale if acquisition and sale are **not more than** ten years apart. Buildings put up or extended in the period are included.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 184 | - **Own-use exception**: used **only** for own housing throughout, **or** for own housing in the year of sale and the two years before (the statute does not say "full" years). A flat let up to the sale meets neither. | [178] / ['- **Own-use exception**: used **only** for own housing between acquisition (or completion) and sale, **or** for own housing in the year of sale and the two years before. A flat let up to the sale meets neither.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 185 | - **Depreciation comes back**: the cost is **reduced** by AfA, increased and special depreciation deducted (§ 23(3) sentence 4). Gain = price less reduced cost less selling costs. | [179] / ['- **Depreciation comes back**: the cost is **reduced** by AfA, increased depreciation and special depreciation claimed (§ 23(3) sentence 4). Gain = price less reduced cost less selling costs.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 186 | - **Exemption limit**: tax-free only if all private-sale gains in the year are **less than** EUR 1,000; otherwise all is taxed. | [180] / ['- **Exemption limit**: tax-free only if all private-sale gains in the year total **less than** EUR 1,000; at EUR 1,000 or more, all is taxed. Losses offset only private-sale gains of the same year, and carry back or forward only against such gains (§ 23(3) sentences 7 and 8).'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 187 | - **Gift**: a single successor takes the donor's acquisition date (§ 23(1) sentence 3); heirs are not named: ask the accountant. | [181] / ["- **Gift**: a single successor who receives it free takes over the donor's acquisition date (§ 23(1) sentence 3). Heirs' communities are not named there: ask the accountant."] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 188 | - **Also**: deduct any rest of a § 82b spread, and check § 7b claw-back. More in `de-capital-gains`. | [182] / ['- **Also**: deduct any rest of a § 82b spread in the year of sale, and check the § 7b claw-back.'] |

| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 194 | \| Completed 31 Dec 2022 vs 1 Jan 2023 \| 2% vs 3% ([§ 7(4) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__7.html)) \| | [84] / ['- **Completion date decides the straight-line rate**, not the purchase date: 3% after 2022, 2% for 1925 to 2022, 2.5% before 1925 (§ 7(4) sentence 1 no. 2). This covers every privately held building, housing or not, including flats and separate parts of a building (§ 7(5b)). The separate 3% rate in no. 1 is for business-asset buildings not used for housing: never use it for a private shop or office completed before 2023.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 195 | \| Construction started 30 Sep 2023 vs 1 Oct 2023, or on or after 1 Oct 2029 \| Declining-balance closed / open / closed (§ 7(5a)) \| | [88] / ['- **Declining-balance AfA, § 7(5a).** Instead of 3% straight-line, 5% of the remaining value ("Restwert") each year, so far as the building is used for **housing**, if all hold: it is in Germany, the EU or the EEA; the client built it, or bought it by the end of the completion year; and construction started (the date on the construction-start notice required by state law; where none is required, the client declares a voluntary notice) or the binding purchase contract was made after 30 September 2023 and before 1 October 2029. No extraordinary write-down while it runs. Switching to straight-line is allowed, then on the remaining value and remaining life.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 196 | \| Mixed building, declining-balance \| Only the housing part (§ 7(5a)) \| | [88] / ['- **Declining-balance AfA, § 7(5a).** Instead of 3% straight-line, 5% of the remaining value ("Restwert") each year, so far as the building is used for **housing**, if all hold: it is in Germany, the EU or the EEA; the client built it, or bought it by the end of the completion year; and construction started (the date on the construction-start notice required by state law; where none is required, the client declares a voluntary notice) or the binding purchase contract was made after 30 September 2023 and before 1 October 2029. No extraordinary write-down while it runs. Switching to straight-line is allowed, then on the remaining value and remaining life.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 197 | \| § 7b application in 2022 \| No special depreciation (§ 7b(2)) \| | [99] / ['- The building work creates **new** flats that did not exist before, under a building application or notice in the **old window** (after 31 August 2018 and before 1 January 2022) or the **new window** (after 31 December 2022 and before 1 October 2029). Nothing applied for in 2022 qualifies.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 198 | \| § 7b cost exactly EUR 5,200 per m² \| Qualifies ("nicht übersteigen"); above it nothing does \| | [100] / ['- Cost per m² of living space **not more than** the cliff (EUR 3,000 old window, EUR 5,200 new window), or no § 7b at all. The base is capped per m² (EUR 2,000 old, EUR 4,000 new). Normal AfA still runs on full cost (BMF letter of 21 May 2025, para. 65).'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 199 | \| § 7b old window in 2027 \| No claim (§ 52(15a)) \| | [104] / ['- **Old window ends with 2026**: claims are allowed "letztmalig für den Veranlagungszeitraum 2026" ([§ 52(15a)](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__52.html)), even if four years have not run. A buyer who started in 2024 claims 2024 to 2026 and loses the fourth year (BMF letter, para. 16, example 1). The new window has no such end.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 200 | \| Works within three years at exactly 15% \| Not over: normal rules (§ 6(1) no. 1a) \| | [119] / ['- **The 15% rule, acquisition-related production cost** ([§ 6(1) no. 1a EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__6.html), applied to Werbungskosten by § 9(5) sentence 2). Repair and modernisation works carried out **within three years** of acquisition become production cost if their total cost **without VAT** "15 Prozent der Anschaffungskosten des Gebäudes übersteigen". It is a cliff: over it, **all** of them are capitalised. The three years are added up, so a later job can pull earlier deductions back in. Exactly 15% is not over. Not counted: extensions, and maintenance that usually recurs every year, such as servicing.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 201 | \| Building half housing, half office by floor area \| Not "mainly" housing: no § 82b spread \| | [121] / ['- **Spreading, [§ 82b EStDV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estdv_1955/__82b.html).** Larger maintenance may be spread evenly over two to five years if, **when paid**, the building is not a business asset and is **mainly** housing: housing floor area more than half the usable area; garages count as housing up to one car per flat. On sale, transfer into a business or end of letting, the rest is deducted that year. Co-owners must use the same period. Works in redevelopment areas and on listed buildings (§§ 11a, 11b EStG, §§ 7h, 7i): refer.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 202 | \| Rent incl. Umlagen just under half / exactly half / exactly 66% of market rent \| Split / forecast band / fully paid (§ 21(2)) \| | [133, 134, 135] / ['\| Less than 50% \| Split into a paid and an unpaid part; costs deductible only for the paid part \|', '\| At least 50% and less than 66% \| Under the published 2025 ELSTER instruction, a forecast with no loss, including exactly zero, gives full cost deduction; a forecast loss requires splitting. This is the dated administrative method used here on the unchanged 2026 statutory band; the older BMF para. 13 uses positive-surplus wording. \|', '\| At least 66%, permanent home let \| Deemed fully paid; all costs deductible \|'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 203 | \| Cheap let of an office to a relative \| Outside § 21(2): refer \| | [147] / ['- **Outside § 21(2)**: commercial space; the 66% sentence covers only permanent home lets. A cheap let of an office to a relative: refer.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 204 | \| Sale exactly ten years after acquisition \| Still inside ("nicht mehr als zehn Jahre") \| | [177] / ['- A sale of land or a flat is a private sale if acquisition and sale are **not more than** ten years apart. Buildings put up or extended in the period are included.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 205 | \| Total private-sale gains EUR 999 vs EUR 1,000 \| Tax-free vs fully taxed (§ 23(3)) \| | [180] / ['- **Exemption limit**: tax-free only if all private-sale gains in the year total **less than** EUR 1,000; at EUR 1,000 or more, all is taxed. Losses offset only private-sale gains of the same year, and carry back or forward only against such gains (§ 23(3) sentences 7 and 8).'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 206 | \| Loss in the year the building is bought \| Not used for that year's prepayments unless § 7b is claimed (§ 37(3)) \| | [197] / ["- **Prepayments** ([§ 37 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__37.html)): 10 March, 10 June, 10 September, 10 December; set only if at least EUR 400 a year and EUR 100 per date. A building's rental loss reduces prepayments only for calendar years beginning **after** its acquisition or completion (§ 37(3) sentence 8); a building bought before the year it is completed counts from completion (sentence 9); the bar does not apply where § 7b is claimed (sentence 10). The same timing governs a wage tax allowance for an employee ([§ 39a(1) no. 5 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__39a.html)). For example, a landlord who bought a new flat in 2026 with a EUR 10,050 loss cannot cut her 2026 prepayments for it; she can from 2027, or at once if § 7b is claimed."] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 207 | \| Employee, untaxed income EUR 410 vs EUR 411 \| No duty from this trigger vs must file (§ 46(2) no. 1) \| | [194] / ['- **Who must file.** A landlord who is assessed anyway files Anlage V with the return. An employee whose wage tax was withheld must file if untaxed side income, such as rent, is **more than** EUR 410 ([§ 46(2) no. 1 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__46.html)); if assessed, side income of not more than EUR 410 in total is taken off again, phased out above (§ 46(3), (5)).'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 208 | \| Let to a private tenant with VAT \| Not possible (§ 9(1) UStG) \| | [166] / ['Letting land and buildings is VAT-exempt (§ 4 no. 12 sentence 1 letter a UStG), except short-term guest rooms, parking spaces, short-term campsites and operating fixtures. The landlord may opt to tax only on a let **to another business for its business** ([§ 9(1) UStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__9.html)), and for land only so far as the tenant uses it **only** for sales that allow input VAT deduction (§ 9(2)). Never to a private tenant. Some buildings begun before 1 June 1984 or 11 November 1993 escape that test ([§ 27(2) UStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__27.html)). If opted: VAT at 19% of net rent, input VAT recoverable; on the income tax return VAT received is income (line 27) and input VAT a cost (lines 80 to 82).'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 210 | ## Worked cases | [232] / ['## Worked examples'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 212 | Inputs are invented and labelled, except the official example in Case 3. Every amount is recomputed. | [234] / ['Inputs are invented and labelled, except Example 2, which is the official example in the 2025 instructions. Each result is the rental surplus or loss before the tariff.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 214 | ### Case 1: new flat, first year ([§ 7 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__7.html)) | [92] / ['- **For example**: completed February 2026, bought with benefit and burden from 1 April 2026, building share EUR 300,000. Declining-balance: 300,000 × 5% × 9/12 = EUR 11,250 in 2026; 2027: (300,000 − 11,250) × 5% = EUR 14,437.50. Straight-line instead: 300,000 × 3% × 9/12 = EUR 6,750. The binding contract was made in March 2026. Total acquisition cost is EUR 400,000, including land of EUR 100,000. From April, rent is EUR 1,200 plus Umlagen of EUR 250 a month: income is 9 × (1,200 + 250) = EUR 13,050. Interest of EUR 9,000, running costs of EUR 2,250 and management of EUR 600 total EUR 11,850. The declining-balance loss is 13,050 − 11,850 − 11,250 = EUR 10,050; with straight-line AfA the loss is EUR 5,550.'] |

| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 223 | ### Case 2: the 15% cliff ([§ 6(1) no. 1a EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__6.html)) | [120] / ['- **For example**: building completed 1970, bought May 2025, building share EUR 200,000, so the limit is EUR 30,000. Works without VAT: EUR 12,000 in 2025 (deducted on the 2025 return) and EUR 19,000 in 2026. Total EUR 31,000 is over EUR 30,000: all of it is production cost, including 2025, and is depreciated at 2% with the building: EUR 31,000 × 2% = EUR 620 for a full year. Refer the correction of the 2025 assessment. With 2026 works of EUR 18,000 the total is exactly EUR 30,000: not over, judge each job on its own.'] |

| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 232 | Cold rent EUR 270 plus Umlagen EUR 150 a month; market rent 60 m² × EUR 15 = EUR 900 plus EUR 150 = EUR 1,050. | [270] / ["A married couple own a 60 m² flat and let it to the wife's parents for EUR 270 cold rent plus EUR 150 Umlagen a month. | pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 233 | - Paid share EUR 420 / EUR 1,050 = 40.00%, under 50%: split. Line 87: 60.00%. | [272] / ['Rent paid including Umlagen is EUR 420, which is 40.00% of EUR 1,050: under 50%, so costs are split. The form shows income EUR 5,040 (line 32), costs in full EUR 4,920 (line 83), result before the cut EUR 120 (line 85), and the cut of 60.00% in line 87. After the cut only EUR 1,968 of costs is deductible, so the taxable surplus is EUR 3,072, EUR 1,536 for each spouse. Variants: rent of EUR 700 including Umlagen is 66.67%, fully paid; EUR 600 is 57.14%, in the forecast band: run the 30-year forecast under "Cheap lets".'] |

| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 238 | ### Case 4: § 7b, new window ([§ 7b EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__7b.html)) | [108] / ['- **For example**: an 80 m² flat applied for in 2024, completed and bought January 2026, Effizienzhaus 40 with the seal, let long-term, cost of the flat without land EUR 400,000 (land never enters § 7b: BMF letter, para. 43). 400,000 / 80 = EUR 5,000 per m², not over 5,200: qualifies. Base cap 80 × 4,000 = EUR 320,000; § 7b 320,000 × 5% = EUR 16,000 a year for 2026 to 2029, plus straight-line 400,000 × 3% = EUR 12,000 (January, no cut). At EUR 420,000 (EUR 5,250 per m²) there is no § 7b.'] |

| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 246 | ### Case 5: sale inside ten years ([§ 23 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__23.html)) | [183] / ['- **For example**: bought March 2019 for EUR 300,000 (building EUR 240,000 at 2% = EUR 4,800 a year); sold at the end of February 2026 for EUR 340,000; selling costs EUR 10,000. AfA claimed: 2019, ten months = EUR 4,000; 2020 to 2025, 6 × 4,800 = EUR 28,800; 2026, two months to the sale, assumed EUR 800. Total EUR 33,600. Reduced cost EUR 266,400; gain 340,000 − 266,400 − 10,000 = EUR 63,600, all taxed. Confirm the sale-year AfA with the accountant. More in `de-capital-gains`.'] |

| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 252 | ### Case 6: new landlord's prepayments ([§ 37 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__37.html)) | [197] / ["- **Prepayments** ([§ 37 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__37.html)): 10 March, 10 June, 10 September, 10 December; set only if at least EUR 400 a year and EUR 100 per date. A building's rental loss reduces prepayments only for calendar years beginning **after** its acquisition or completion (§ 37(3) sentence 8); a building bought before the year it is completed counts from completion (sentence 9); the bar does not apply where § 7b is claimed (sentence 10). The same timing governs a wage tax allowance for an employee ([§ 39a(1) no. 5 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__39a.html)). For example, a landlord who bought a new flat in 2026 with a EUR 10,050 loss cannot cut her 2026 prepayments for it; she can from 2027, or at once if § 7b is claimed."] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 254 | The Case 1 client asks in autumn 2026 to cut 2026 prepayments for her EUR 10,050 loss. | [197] / ["- **Prepayments** ([§ 37 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__37.html)): 10 March, 10 June, 10 September, 10 December; set only if at least EUR 400 a year and EUR 100 per date. A building's rental loss reduces prepayments only for calendar years beginning **after** its acquisition or completion (§ 37(3) sentence 8); a building bought before the year it is completed counts from completion (sentence 9); the bar does not apply where § 7b is claimed (sentence 10). The same timing governs a wage tax allowance for an employee ([§ 39a(1) no. 5 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__39a.html)). For example, a landlord who bought a new flat in 2026 with a EUR 10,050 loss cannot cut her 2026 prepayments for it; she can from 2027, or at once if § 7b is claimed."] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 255 | - Refused for 2026: the building was acquired in 2026 (§ 37(3) sentence 8). Allowed from 2027.
