| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 256 | - Her adviser-prepared 2025 return is due Monday 1 March 2027. | [196] / ['- **2025 returns being filed now.** Without an adviser: due Friday 31 July 2026, passed. With an adviser: the last day of February 2027 is a Sunday, so **Monday 1 March 2027**. The extended dates in Art. 97 § 36(3) EGAO cover only "die Besteuerungszeiträume 2020 bis 2024" ([EGAO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/aoeg_1977/art_97__36.html)). The advised 2024 return was due 30 April 2026 under that transitional rule. Use the 2025 Anlage V. Rules are as for 2026, except the commuting allowance: if a first place of activity applies, the 2025 rate was EUR 0.30 per full one-way kilometre for the first 20 km and EUR 0.38 for each further kilometre (2025 instructions); from 2026 it is EUR 0.38 from the first kilometre. 2025 is the second-last year for the old § 7b window.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 258 | ## When to refuse or refer | [30, 344] / ["- **Refer, not covered here**: co-ownership determinations (separate and uniform assessment), heirs' communities, funds, usufruct (Nießbrauch), heritable building rights (Erbbaurecht) as a party, §§ 7h and 7i, and §§ 15a and 15b loss limits.", 'Not yet confirmed: the 2026 Anlage V form and instructions (the recorded route returned no substantive content; line numbers are from 2025); any BMF update to the letter of 8 October 2004 after the retained 2024 handbook (the checked 2025 handbook route remained inaccessible); the BMF letter of 22 February 2023 on a shorter actual useful life; whether a let property can be a first place of activity for travel; AfA in the month of sale; heirs under § 23(1) sentence 3; treaty treatment of foreign rental property; the BMF letter of 21 October 2026 on paid waiver of usufruct (Nießbrauch), not read.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 260 | - Business property, trading partnerships, or a possible trade in property (gewerblicher Grundstückshandel). | [25, 30] / ['- **Not here: letting that belongs to another kind of income** (§ 21(3) EStG). Property held in a business, a trading partnership, or a trade in property (gewerblicher Grundstückshandel) is taxed as business income: refer, and see `de-trade-tax`. Hotel-like services can also make a let a trade.', "- **Refer, not covered here**: co-ownership determinations (separate and uniform assessment), heirs' communities, funds, usufruct (Nießbrauch), heritable building rights (Erbbaurecht) as a party, §§ 7h and 7i, and §§ 15a and 15b loss limits."] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 261 | - Non-resident landlords: compute the rental result here, then tax it under `de-non-resident-tax`. Foreign property (Anlage AUS, treaties): refer. | [27, 227, 228] / ['- **Non-residents with German property** are taxed on German rent under [§ 49(1) no. 6 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__49.html). Compute the rental result with this skill, then go to `de-non-resident-tax` for liability, the tariff and filing.', '- **Non-resident landlords** with German property are taxed on the rent ([§ 49(1) no. 6 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__49.html)). They deduct only Werbungskosten linked to the German income ([§ 50(1) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__50.html)), and their tariff is applied with the basic allowance added back unless an option applies. Compute the rental result here; liability, the § 1(3) and § 1a options, and filing are in `de-non-resident-tax`.', '- **German residents with property abroad**: report the rental income and determine the treatment from the actual treaty for the property country. Treaty exemption, credit, progression and Anlage AUS: refer.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 262 | - The cheap-let forecast band; doubted intention to make a surplus; lasting losses. | [139, 153, 154, 161] / ['- **The forecast (Totalüberschussprognose)** for the 50% to 66% band, per the BMF letter of 8 October 2004 (BStBl I p. 933), paras. 13 and 34, as printed with its notes in the [official income tax handbook 2024, Anhang 30 II](https://esth.bundesfinanzministerium.de/esth/2024/C-Anhaenge/Anhang-30/II/anhang-30-II.html). The notes state that from 2021 para. 13 applies with 50% and 66% in place of 56% and 75%.', '- **Long-term home lets.** A let of a flat meant to last is taken to aim at a surplus without a test (para. 1). That presumption does not cover commercial premises or undeveloped land (note to para. 1): there the forecast below decides.', '- **Signs against the intention** (paras. 5 to 7): a let planned only for a time (a fixed-term lease with stated own use or sale, short financing, an early search for a buyer), or a sale or own use within about five years of acquisition with only losses in that time. The client can rebut by showing the decision to sell or move in came later (para. 9).', '- **Without the intention** the losses are not recognised (Liebhaberei). If the lack of intention only shows later, final assessments can be changed under § 173(1) no. 1 AO (para. 10).'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 263 | - Holiday homes with own use; short-term lets with hotel-like services. | [158, 159, 160, 162] / ['- **Holiday flat let only to changing guests** and otherwise held ready for them: the intention is taken as given without a test, whether the client lets it directly or through an agent (para. 16). Exception: if letting days fall short of the usual local letting time by **at least 25%**, with no obstacle to letting, a forecast is needed (note to para. 16).', "- **Proving there is no own use** (para. 17): the client shows one of: letting handed to an unrelated agent with own use excluded by contract for the whole year; the flat is in or right next to the client's own home, which meets the client's needs; the client has more than one holiday flat in the place and uses only one; or letting days at least equal the local seasonal average. Otherwise the client must prove it, and with too few letting days show stronger advertising (para. 18). Short stays for servicing, key handover, cleaning, checks, repairs or owners' meetings are not own use; longer stays or stays with family need explaining (para. 19).", '- **Holiday flat let part of the time and used by the owner** (or reserved for own use, or lent free): the intention is always tested by forecast (para. 21), per flat, not per let (para. 34). Only costs for let days and vacancy allocated to letting go in (para. 39); mixed costs (interest, property charges, maintenance, AfA, insurance) are split by days of each use (para. 40). Vacancy: if own use is limited to set times, only those count as own use; if possible at any time, split vacancy by the ratio of own use to letting; if the extent of own use cannot be established, vacancy is split 50/50 (paras. 22, 23). The forecast then follows para. 34: 30 years, § 7(4) AfA, five-year average, 10% margins (see the cheap-let forecast above).', '- **Trade and VAT.** Hotel-like services may make it a trade (`de-trade-tax`). Short-term guest accommodation is not VAT-exempt ([§ 4 no. 12 sentence 2 UStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__4.html)); where VAT is due it is at 7% ([§ 12(2) no. 11 UStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__12.html)), except for services not directly part of the letting, such as breakfast, even if included in the price. Whether VAT is due turns on the small-business rule: `germany-vat-return`. No § 7b for short-term lets.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 264 | - Shorter actual useful life; change of method on older buildings. | [85, 86, 344] / ['- **Shorter actual life.** If the actual life is under 33 years (after 2022), 50 years (1925 to 2022) or 40 years (before 1925), that life may be used (§ 7(4) sentence 2). The 2025 instructions call this an exceptional case that must be proved to the tax office. Refer.', '- **Older staged rates.** A building built or bought in its completion year under an application or contract before 1 January 2006 (housing; earlier dates for the other historic branches) may be on the staged rates of § 7(5). Ask what past returns used; refer any change.', 'Not yet confirmed: the 2026 Anlage V form and instructions (the recorded route returned no substantive content; line numbers are from 2025); any BMF update to the letter of 8 October 2004 after the retained 2024 handbook (the checked 2025 handbook route remained inaccessible); the BMF letter of 22 February 2023 on a shorter actual useful life; whether a let property can be a first place of activity for travel; AfA in the month of sale; heirs under § 23(1) sentence 3; treaty treatment of foreign rental property; the BMF letter of 21 October 2026 on paid waiver of usufruct (Nießbrauch), not read.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 265 | - Doubtful § 7b cases, claw-back, the post-four-year computation; §§ 7h, 7i. | [30, 105, 106] / ["- **Refer, not covered here**: co-ownership determinations (separate and uniform assessment), heirs' communities, funds, usufruct (Nießbrauch), heritable building rights (Erbbaurecht) as a party, §§ 7h and 7i, and §§ 15a and 15b loss limits.", '- **Claw-back** of all past claims if the flat is not let for housing for ten years, is sold in that time with the gain untaxed, or later costs push it over the cliff within three years after the year of acquisition or production (§ 7b(4)).', '- **After the four-year benefit period**, regular AfA uses the remaining value and the § 7(4) percentage determined from the remaining mathematical useful life; for § 7(5a), apply its 5% rate to that remaining value ([§ 7a(9) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__7a.html); BMF letter, para. 66). The old-window 2026 sunset does not shorten that four-year period: the 2024 buyer above still uses unchanged regular AfA in 2027 and changes to § 7a(9) in 2028 (BMF para. 16). Establish the actual historical normal/special deductions and useful-life branch before computing; do not substitute a guessed partial-year balance.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 266 | - Correcting an earlier year after the three-year works rule is triggered. | [120] / ['- **For example**: building completed 1970, bought May 2025, building share EUR 200,000, so the limit is EUR 30,000. Works without VAT: EUR 12,000 in 2025 (deducted on the 2025 return) and EUR 19,000 in 2026. Total EUR 31,000 is over EUR 30,000: all of it is production cost, including 2025, and is depreciated at 2% with the building: EUR 31,000 × 2% = EUR 620 for a full year. Refer the correction of the 2025 assessment. With 2026 works of EUR 18,000 the total is exactly EUR 30,000: not over, judge each job on its own.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 267 | - Co-ownership determinations, heirs' communities, funds, usufruct (Nießbrauch), heritable building rights (Erbbaurecht). | [30] / ["- **Refer, not covered here**: co-ownership determinations (separate and uniform assessment), heirs' communities, funds, usufruct (Nießbrauch), heritable building rights (Erbbaurecht) as a party, §§ 7h and 7i, and §§ 15a and 15b loss limits."] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 268 | - §§ 15a, 15b loss limits; furniture surcharges in the market-rent test; cheap commercial lets. | [125, 147, 173, 344] / ['A furniture surcharge is rent. Furniture and fitted kitchens are depreciated over their useful life (§ 7(1) EStG; lines 42 to 45). An item usable on its own costing not more than EUR 800 net may be deducted at once ([§ 6(2) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__6.html) through § 9(1) no. 7). For the cheap-let comparison, use a furniture adjustment already provided by the local rent index without adding it twice. Otherwise add a surcharge only if it can be established from the local market for furnished lets; if no market value can be determined, no surcharge is added. Never derive it from monthly furniture AfA or a standard investment return ([BFH, 6 February 2018, IX R 14/17, paras. 18 to 20](https://www.bundesfinanzhof.de/de/entscheidung/entscheidungen-online/detail/pdf/STRE201810105?type=1646225765)).', '- **Outside § 21(2)**: commercial space; the 66% sentence covers only permanent home lets. A cheap let of an office to a relative: refer.', '- **Loss schemes**: §§ 15a and 15b apply by analogy (§ 21(1) sentence 2). Refer.', 'Not yet confirmed: the 2026 Anlage V form and instructions (the recorded route returned no substantive content; line numbers are from 2025); any BMF update to the letter of 8 October 2004 after the retained 2024 handbook (the checked 2025 handbook route remained inaccessible); the BMF letter of 22 February 2023 on a shorter actual useful life; whether a let property can be a first place of activity for travel; AfA in the month of sale; heirs under § 23(1) sentence 3; treaty treatment of foreign rental property; the BMF letter of 21 October 2026 on paid waiver of usufruct (Nießbrauch), not read.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 269 | - Sale computations beyond the ten-year test (`de-capital-gains`); VAT beyond the basics (`germany-vat-return`); the tariff (`de-einkommensteuer-freelancer`). | [31, 177, 227, 228] / ['- **Related skills.** `de-capital-gains` (sales), `germany-vat-return` and `de-vat-annual-and-special-schemes` (VAT), `de-trade-tax` (a letting that is a trade), `de-estimated-tax` (prepayments), `de-employee-income-tax` (employees who must file), `de-non-resident-tax` (non-resident landlords).', '- A sale of land or a flat is a private sale if acquisition and sale are **not more than** ten years apart. Buildings put up or extended in the period are included.', '- **Non-resident landlords** with German property are taxed on the rent ([§ 49(1) no. 6 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__49.html)). They deduct only Werbungskosten linked to the German income ([§ 50(1) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__50.html)), and their tariff is applied with the basic allowance added back unless an option applies. Compute the rental result here; liability, the § 1(3) and § 1a options, and filing are in `de-non-resident-tax`.', '- **German residents with property abroad**: report the rental income and determine the treatment from the actual treaty for the property country. Treaty exemption, credit, progression and Anlage AUS: refer.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 271 | ## Filing and payment | [191] / ['## Deadlines and filing'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 273 | - **Return**: Anlage V per property, plus V-FeWo and V-Sonstige as needed, through ELSTER. | [193] / ['- **Forms.** One Anlage V per built-on property, filed with the income tax return through ELSTER. Add Anlage V-FeWo for holiday or short-term lets, and Anlage V-Sonstige for shares in communities and funds, subletting, land without buildings and rights (2025 instructions).'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 274 | - **Employees**: must file if untaxed income (rent counts) is **more than** EUR 410; if assessed, such income of **not more than** EUR 410 in total is taken off again, phased out above ([§ 46(2) no. 1, (3), (5) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__46.html)). One trigger among several. | [194] / ['- **Who must file.** A landlord who is assessed anyway files Anlage V with the return. An employee whose wage tax was withheld must file if untaxed side income, such as rent, is **more than** EUR 410 ([§ 46(2) no. 1 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__46.html)); if assessed, side income of not more than EUR 410 in total is taken off again, phased out above (§ 46(3), (5)).'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 275 | - **Deadlines** ([§ 149 AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__149.html)): unadvised, seven months after the year; for 2026 that is 31 July 2027, a Saturday, so Monday 2 August 2027. Advised: last day of February of the second following year, for 2026 Tuesday 29 February 2028; the tax office may call for it earlier (§ 149(4)). Weekend and holiday deadlines move to the next working day ([§ 108(3) AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__108.html)). | [195] / ['- **2026 return** ([§ 149 AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__149.html)): without an adviser, seven months after the year: 31 July 2027 is a Saturday, so **Monday 2 August 2027** ([§ 108(3) AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__108.html)). With an adviser, the last day of February of the second following year: **Tuesday 29 February 2028**. The tax office may call for an advised return earlier (§ 149(4)).'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 276 | - **Late filing** ([§ 152 AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__152.html)): compulsory if the return is filed "nicht binnen 14 Monaten nach Ablauf des Kalenderjahrs" (§ 152(2) no. 1), advised or not, so for an advised return it applies from the day after the advised deadline; also compulsory if a return the office called in early is not filed by the date set (no. 3). The 19-month period in no. 2 is only for farmers and foresters with a non-calendar financial year (§ 149(2) sentence 2), not for advised returns. It is 0.25% a month of the tax less prepayments and withheld tax, at least EUR 25 per month or part month. Not compulsory (but still possible at discretion) if the deadline was extended, tax is nil or below, or tax does not exceed prepayments and withheld tax (§ 152(3)). | [220] / ['- **Late-filing surcharge** ([§ 152 AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__152.html)). Compulsory if the return is not filed "binnen 14 Monaten nach Ablauf des Kalenderjahrs" (§ 152(2) no. 1), advised or not. For 2026 the 14 months end on 29 February 2028, the same day as the advised deadline, so for an advised return it bites from the day after that deadline. Also compulsory if a return the office called in early is not filed by the date set (no. 3). The 19-month period in no. 2 is only for farmers with a non-calendar financial year. Amount: 0.25% a month of the tax less prepayments and withheld tax, at least EUR 25 for each started month (§ 152(5)), rounded down to full euros and capped at EUR 25,000 (§ 152(10)). Not compulsory, though still possible at discretion, if the deadline was extended, the tax is nil or negative, or the tax does not exceed prepayments and withheld tax (§ 152(3)). For an unadvised 2025 return filed now the surcharge is discretionary until the 14 months run out at the end of February 2027.'] |

The extended dates in Art. 97 § 36(3) EGAO cover only "die Besteuerungszeiträume 2020 bis 2024" ([EGAO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/aoeg_1977/art_97__36.html)). The advised 2024 return was due 30 April 2026 under that transitional rule. Use the 2025 Anlage V. Rules are as for 2026, except the commuting allowance: if a first place of activity applies, the 2025 rate was EUR 0.30 per full one-way kilometre for the first 20 km and EUR 0.38 for each further kilometre (2025 instructions); from 2026 it is EUR 0.38 from the first kilometre. 2025 is the second-last year for the old § 7b window.', '- **2026 forms.** The ELSTER 2026 instructions route returned no substantive content in the recorded 24 October 2026 check; this does not establish whether the instructions were published. Line numbers below come from the [2025 instructions](https://www.elster.de/eportal/helpGlobal?themaGlobal=help_est_ufa_10_2025). Confirm the relevant 2026 form before using these line numbers.'] |

| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 303 | The instructions name the content of lines 46 to 51 and 55 to 72, the Hausgeld settlement (73 to 75) and, in the official example, account fees (76 to 78); other keyword placements are this skill's reading of the headings and do not change the total in line 83. **Exclude**: principal (TILGUNG), own transfers, deposit refunds, reserve payments until spent, personal taxes. Transfer tax (GRUNDERWERBSTEUER) goes into acquisition cost. | [216] / ["The instructions name the content of lines 25, 26, 46 to 51, 55 to 72, the Hausgeld settlement (73 to 75) and, in the official example, account fees (76 to 78). Other keyword placements are this skill's reading of the headings and do not change the total in line 83. **Exclude**: principal (TILGUNG), own transfers, deposit refunds, reserve payments until spent, personal taxes. Transfer tax (GRUNDERWERBSTEUER) goes into acquisition cost."] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 305 | ## Completion checklist | [187, 189, 305] / ['Fix the building share before the three-year 15% test, which measures against it. Complete the costs before the cheap-let cut, which applies to their total. Then work out the surplus or loss per property, share it between co-owners, and apply the loss rules.', '**Defaults when a fact is missing.** Completion date, land share, market rent for a cheap let, or private versus business status unknown: stop and ask, because each changes the result. Unsure whether work is maintenance or production cost: treat it as production cost (capitalise) until resolved. Housing use unknown: ask, because § 7(5a), § 7b, § 21(2) and § 82b apply only to housing.', '## Ask the client first'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 307 | - [ ] § 21 income from a private asset; right forms and form year. | [24, 193, 198] / ['- **Individuals letting land, buildings, flats or rooms held as private assets.** This is income from letting and leasing under [§ 21(1) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__21.html): immovable property (land, buildings, parts of buildings, rights such as a heritable building right), bundles of movable assets, the temporary grant of rights, and the sale of rent claims.', '- **Forms.** One Anlage V per built-on property, filed with the income tax return through ELSTER. Add Anlage V-FeWo for holiday or short-term lets, and Anlage V-Sonstige for shares in communities and funds, subletting, land without buildings and rights (2025 instructions).', '- **2026 forms.** The ELSTER 2026 instructions route returned no substantive content in the recorded 24 October 2026 check; this does not establish whether the instructions were published. Line numbers below come from the [2025 instructions](https://www.elster.de/eportal/helpGlobal?themaGlobal=help_est_ufa_10_2025). Confirm the relevant 2026 form before using these line numbers.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 308 | - [ ] Cash-basis income incl. Umlagen, settlements, deposits kept, VAT. | [67, 69] / ['- **Income**: rent; Umlagen (running costs paid by the tenant); settlement back-payments; a deposit kept and set off against rent, running costs or damage; payments for antenna, wind or Wi-Fi sites; a furniture surcharge; VAT received on an opted let; grants for rent (2025 instructions, lines 13 to 31). A refundable deposit is not income while held.', '- **Cash basis** ([§ 11 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__11.html)): income when received, costs when paid. Regularly recurring items paid shortly before or after the turn of the year belong to the year they relate to. Rent received in advance for a let of more than five years **may** be spread over the period; costs paid in advance for more than five years **must** be spread. A market-level loan discount (Disagio) is deductible when paid.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 309 | - [ ] Land split off by market values; rate by completion date; declining-balance conditions; month cut; no business-asset rate. | [84, 88, 89, 90, 91] / ['- **Completion date decides the straight-line rate**, not the purchase date: 3% after 2022, 2% for 1925 to 2022, 2.5% before 1925 (§ 7(4) sentence 1 no. 2). This covers every privately held building, housing or not, including flats and separate parts of a building (§ 7(5b)). The separate 3% rate in no. 1 is for business-asset buildings not used for housing: never use it for a private shop or office completed before 2023.', '- **Declining-balance AfA, § 7(5a).** Instead of 3% straight-line, 5% of the remaining value ("Restwert") each year, so far as the building is used for **housing**, if all hold: it is in Germany, the EU or the EEA; the client built it, or bought it by the end of the completion year; and construction started (the date on the construction-start notice required by state law; where none is required, the client declares a voluntary notice) or the binding purchase contract was made after 30 September 2023 and before 1 October 2029. No extraordinary write-down while it runs. Switching to straight-line is allowed, then on the remaining value and remaining life.', "- **Base.** Price plus side costs (transfer tax, notary, land register, the buyer's broker) under [§ 255 HGB](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/hgb/__255.html), less the land share. Land is never depreciated. A maintenance reserve bought with a flat is excluded (2025 instructions). Grants reduce the base (line 89). A former own home let for the first time needs a first computation.", "- **Price split.** Split by the ratio of market values, not by the residual-value method (2025 instructions). The [BMF purchase-price split tool](https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/Steuern/Berechnung-Aufteilung-Grundstueckskaufpreis.html) makes or tests a split. A contract split at arm's length or an expert valuation may also be used; the tax office may challenge a high building share.", '- **First-year month cut.** Cut one twelfth for each full month before the month of acquisition or completion (§ 7(1) sentence 4). Statute applies it to § 7(5a) (sentence 5). For straight-line AfA, the BMF letter of 21 May 2025 on § 7b says so too: "bei Fertigstellung nach dem 31. Januar wäre eine zeitanteilige Kürzung der linearen AfA im Jahr der Fertigstellung erforderlich" (example 7). Only the old staged § 7(5) rates take a full first year.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 310 | - [ ] § 7b window, cliff, cap, ten-year let; old window not after 2026. | [97, 99, 100, 102, 104] / ['Up to 5% of the base in the year of acquisition or production and the three following years, on top of normal AfA under § 7(4) or § 7(5a). All must hold:', '- The building work creates **new** flats that did not exist before, under a building application or notice in the **old window** (after 31 August 2018 and before 1 January 2022) or the **new window** (after 31 December 2022 and before 1 October 2029). Nothing applied for in 2022 qualifies.', '- Cost per m² of living space **not more than** the cliff (EUR 3,000 old window, EUR 5,200 new window), or no § 7b at all. The base is capped per m² (EUR 2,000 old, EUR 4,000 new). Normal AfA still runs on full cost (BMF letter of 21 May 2025, para. 65).', '- Let **for housing, against payment**, in that year and the **nine** following years. Short-term guest accommodation is not housing (§ 7b(2) no. 3).', '- **Old window ends with 2026**: claims are allowed "letztmalig für den Veranlagungszeitraum 2026" ([§ 52(15a)](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__52.html)), even if four years have not run. A buyer who started in 2024 claims 2024 to 2026 and loses the fourth year (BMF letter, para. 16, example 1). The new window has no such end.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 311 | - [ ] Three-year works test, without VAT, on building cost; § 82b only if mainly housing; co-owners on one period. | [119, 121] / ['- **The 15% rule, acquisition-related production cost** ([§ 6(1) no. 1a EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__6.html), applied to Werbungskosten by § 9(5) sentence 2). Repair and modernisation works carried out **within three years** of acquisition become production cost if their total cost **without VAT** "15 Prozent der Anschaffungskosten des Gebäudes übersteigen". It is a cliff: over it, **all** of them are capitalised. The three years are added up, so a later job can pull earlier deductions back in. Exactly 15% is not over. Not counted: extensions, and maintenance that usually recurs every year, such as servicing.', '- **Spreading, [§ 82b EStDV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estdv_1955/__82b.html).** Larger maintenance may be spread evenly over two to five years if, **when paid**, the building is not a business asset and is **mainly** housing: housing floor area more than half the usable area; garages count as housing up to one car per flat. On sale, transfer into a business or end of letting, the rest is deducted that year. Co-owners must use the same period. Works in redevelopment areas and on listed buildings (§§ 11a, 11b EStG, §§ 7h, 7i): refer.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 312 | - [ ] No principal, reserve payments or fines deducted. | [77, 78, 79] / ['- **Not deductible**: loan principal (Tilgung); transfer tax, notary and broker on the purchase (they go into acquisition cost); reserve payments until spent; costs attributable to own-use or free-use parts; the unpaid part of a cheap let; fines (§ 9(5) with § 4(5) no. 8 EStG); income tax, solidarity surcharge and church tax.', '', 'For mixed private and rental use, first attribute identifiable costs directly. Allocate shared costs by a justified, documented measure, such as the actual usable-floor-area ratio, and explain a first allocation (published 2025 ELSTER instructions, applied here to the unchanged rental-expense principle, not claimed 2026 form lines). For example, an identifiable rented-room repair of EUR 1,000 plus a shared roof repair of EUR 10,000 at an independently supported 20% rental share gives EUR 3,000 rental costs before any other applicable limitation; private-only costs stay excluded. The 20% is a stipulated allocation fact, not a universal prescribed ratio.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 313 | - [ ] Cheap-let test per home; line 87 or 88; forecast band referred. | [133, 134, 135, 138, 139] / ['\| Less than 50% \| Split into a paid and an unpaid part; costs deductible only for the paid part \|', '\| At least 50% and less than 66% \| Under the published 2025 ELSTER instruction, a forecast with no loss, including exactly zero, gives full cost deduction; a forecast loss requires splitting. This is the dated administrative method used here on the unchanged 2026 statutory band; the older BMF para. 13 uses positive-surplus wording. \|', '\| At least 66%, permanent home let \| Deemed fully paid; all costs deductible \|', '- **The split**: costs in the ratio of agreed rent to market rent. On the 2025 form enter all costs in full (lines 33 to 84) and the cut percentage in line 87, or an amount in line 88 if only part of the property is let cheaply. The tax office makes the cut.', '- **The forecast (Totalüberschussprognose)** for the 50% to 66% band, per the BMF letter of 8 October 2004 (BStBl I p. 933), paras. 13 and 34, as printed with its notes in the [official income tax handbook 2024, Anhang 30 II](https://esth.bundesfinanzministerium.de/esth/2024/C-Anhaenge/Anhang-30/II/anhang-30-II.html). The notes state that from 2021 para. 13 applies with 50% and 66% in place of 56% and 75%.'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 314 | - [ ] Loss offset, carry-back, carry-forward; prepayment rule for new buildings. | [170, 171, 172, 197] / ['- **Same year**: against other rental income, then other kinds of income (§ 2(3) EStG).', '- **Carry-back**: up to EUR 1,000,000 (EUR 2,000,000 joint) to the year before, then the second year before; the client may waive it only in full.', '- **Carry-forward**: no time limit; unlimited up to total income of EUR 1 million (EUR 2 million joint), then 70% of the excess for 2024 to 2027 and 60% from 2028. Article 6 of BGBl. 2024 I No. 108 changes "70 Prozent" back to "60 Prozent" from 1 January 2028; the consolidated text on gesetze-im-internet.de does not show that yet. Fixed by a separate notice (§ 10d(4)).', "- **Prepayments** ([§ 37 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__37.html)): 10 March, 10 June, 10 September, 10 December; set only if at least EUR 400 a year and EUR 100 per date. A building's rental loss reduces prepayments only for calendar years beginning **after** its acquisition or completion (§ 37(3) sentence 8); a building bought before the year it is completed counts from completion (sentence 9); the bar does not apply where § 7b is claimed (sentence 10). The same timing governs a wage tax allowance for an employee ([§ 39a(1) no. 5 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__39a.html)). For example, a landlord who bought a new flat in 2026 with a EUR 10,050 loss cannot cut her 2026 prepayments for it; she can from 2027, or at once if § 7b is claimed."] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 315 | - [ ] Sale: ten years, AfA added back, exemption limit. | [177, 179, 180] / ['- A sale of land or a flat is a private sale if acquisition and sale are **not more than** ten years apart. Buildings put up or extended in the period are included.', '- **Depreciation comes back**: the cost is **reduced** by AfA, increased depreciation and special depreciation claimed (§ 23(3) sentence 4). Gain = price less reduced cost less selling costs.', '- **Exemption limit**: tax-free only if all private-sale gains in the year total **less than** EUR 1,000; at EUR 1,000 or more, all is taxed. Losses offset only private-sale gains of the same year, and carry back or forward only against such gains (§ 23(3) sentences 7 and 8).'] |
| pre-refresh 3e241566^ / 316 | - [ ] Deadline and surcharge checked; results labelled estimates until an accountant signs off. | [20, 195, 220] / ["> **General reference only.** This skill is general tax reference material for AI-assisted work. It has not been reviewed for any specific landlord's facts. A Steuerberater should confirm results before a return is filed.", '- **2026 return** ([§ 149 AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__149.html)): without an adviser, seven months after the year: 31 July 2027 is a Saturday, so **Monday 2 August 2027** ([§ 108(3) AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__108.html)). With an adviser, the last day of February of the second following year: **Tuesday 29 February 2028**. The tax office may call for an advised return earlier (§ 149(4)).', '- **Late-filing surcharge** ([§ 152 AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__152.html)). Compulsory if the return is not filed "binnen 14 Monaten nach Ablauf des Kalenderjahrs" (§ 152(2) no. 1), advised or not. For 2026 the 14 months end on 29 February 2028, the same day as the advised deadline, so for an advised return it bites from the day after that deadline. Also compulsory if a return the office called in early is not filed by the date set (no. 3). The 19-month period in no. 2 is only for farmers with a non-calendar financial year. Amount: 0.25% a month of the tax less prepayments and withheld tax, at least EUR 25 for each started month (§ 152(5)), rounded down to full euros and capped at EUR 25,000 (§ 152(10)). Not compulsory, though still possible at discretion, if the deadline was extended, the tax is nil or negative, or the tax does not exceed prepayments and withheld tax (§ 152(3)). For an unadvised 2025 return filed now the surcharge is discretionary until the 14 months run out at the end of February 2027.'] |

| main62b7639a baseline / 2 | name: de-rental-income | [2] / ['name: de-rental-income'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 3 | description: "German income from letting property held privately (Einkünfte aus Vermietung und Verpachtung, § 21 EStG), tax year 2026. Covers what counts as rent and Werbungskosten (§ 9 EStG), building depreciation at 2%, 2.5% or 3% (§ 7(4)), the 5% declining-balance AfA for new housing (§ 7(5a)) and § 7b special depreciation for new rental flats, maintenance versus acquisition-related production cost (the 15% three-year rule) and § 82b EStDV spreading, cheap lets to relatives (the 50% and 66% rules), holiday and short-term lets and the intention to make a surplus, losses, the ten-year rule on a sale (§ 23), Anlage V and deadlines. Trigger on: Mieteinnahmen, Vermietung, Anlage V, AfA Gebäude, Werbungskosten Vermietung, anschaffungsnahe Herstellungskosten, verbilligte Vermietung, Ferienwohnung, Spekulationsfrist, rental income Germany. Out of scope: property in a business or a trade in property (de-trade-tax), the tariff (de-einkommensteuer-freelancer), non-resident landlords (de-non-resident-tax), sales beyond the ten-year test (de-capital-gains), VAT returns (germany-vat-return)." | [3] / ['description: "German income from letting property held privately (Einkünfte aus Vermietung und Verpachtung, § 21 EStG), tax year 2026. Covers what counts as rent and Werbungskosten (§ 9 EStG), building depreciation at 2%, 2.5% or 3% (§ 7(4)), the 5% declining-balance AfA for new housing (§ 7(5a)) and § 7b special depreciation for new rental flats, maintenance versus acquisition-related production cost (the 15% three-year rule) and § 82b EStDV spreading, cheap lets to relatives (the 50% and 66% rules), holiday and short-term lets and the intention to make a surplus, losses, the ten-year rule on a sale (§ 23), Anlage V and deadlines. Trigger on: Mieteinnahmen, Vermietung, Anlage V, AfA Gebäude, Werbungskosten Vermietung, anschaffungsnahe Herstellungskosten, verbilligte Vermietung, Ferienwohnung, Spekulationsfrist, rental income Germany. Out of scope: property in a business or a trade in property (de-trade-tax), the tariff (de-einkommensteuer-freelancer), non-resident landlords (de-non-resident-tax), sales beyond the ten-year test (de-capital-gains), VAT returns (germany-vat-return)."'] |

| main62b7639a baseline / 22 | ## Who this applies to | [22] / ['## Who this applies to'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 24 | - **Individuals letting land, buildings, flats or rooms held as private assets.** This is income from letting and leasing under [§ 21(1) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__21.html): immovable property (land, buildings, parts of buildings, rights such as a heritable building right), bundles of movable assets, the temporary grant of rights, and the sale of rent claims. | [24] / ['- **Individuals letting land, buildings, flats or rooms held as private assets.** This is income from letting and leasing under [§ 21(1) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__21.html): immovable property (land, buildings, parts of buildings, rights such as a heritable building right), bundles of movable assets, the temporary grant of rights, and the sale of rent claims.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 25 | - **Not here: letting that belongs to another kind of income** (§ 21(3) EStG). Property held in a business, a trading partnership, or a trade in property (gewerblicher Grundstückshandel) is taxed as business income: refer, and see `de-trade-tax`. Hotel-like services can also make a let a trade. | [25] / ['- **Not here: letting that belongs to another kind of income** (§ 21(3) EStG). Property held in a business, a trading partnership, or a trade in property (gewerblicher Grundstückshandel) is taxed as business income: refer, and see `de-trade-tax`. Hotel-like services can also make a let a trade.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 26 | - **Residents.** A person with a home or habitual abode in Germany is taxed on worldwide income. This skill computes the German rental result. Rent from property abroad (Anlage AUS, treaty exemption): refer. | [26] / ['- **Residents.** A person with a home or habitual abode in Germany is taxed on worldwide income. This skill computes the German rental result. Rent from property abroad (Anlage AUS, treaty exemption): refer.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 27 | - **Non-residents with German property** are taxed on German rent under [§ 49(1) no. 6 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__49.html). Compute the rental result with this skill, then go to `de-non-resident-tax` for liability, the tariff and filing. | [27] / ['- **Non-residents with German property** are taxed on German rent under [§ 49(1) no. 6 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__49.html). Compute the rental result with this skill, then go to `de-non-resident-tax` for liability, the tariff and filing.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 28 | - **Year.** Tax year 2026, the calendar year. The consolidated EStG as read applies "erstmals für den Veranlagungszeitraum 2026" ([§ 52(1) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__52.html)). For 2025 returns still being filed, see "Deadlines and filing". | [28] / ['- **Year.** Tax year 2026, the calendar year. The consolidated EStG as read applies "erstmals für den Veranlagungszeitraum 2026" ([§ 52(1) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__52.html)). For 2025 returns still being filed, see "Deadlines and filing".'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 29 | - **Measure.** Receipts less Werbungskosten, on a cash basis. The result joins the client's other income at the normal tariff with solidarity surcharge and church tax: use `de-einkommensteuer-freelancer` for the tariff. | [29] / ["- **Measure.** Receipts less Werbungskosten, on a cash basis. The result joins the client's other income at the normal tariff with solidarity surcharge and church tax: use `de-einkommensteuer-freelancer` for the tariff."] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 30 | - **Refer, not covered here**: co-ownership determinations (separate and uniform assessment), heirs' communities, funds, usufruct (Nießbrauch), heritable building rights (Erbbaurecht) as a party, §§ 7h and 7i, and §§ 15a and 15b loss limits. | [30] / ["- **Refer, not covered here**: co-ownership determinations (separate and uniform assessment), heirs' communities, funds, usufruct (Nießbrauch), heritable building rights (Erbbaurecht) as a party, §§ 7h and 7i, and §§ 15a and 15b loss limits."] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 31 | - **Related skills.** `de-capital-gains` (sales), `germany-vat-return` and `de-vat-annual-and-special-schemes` (VAT), `de-trade-tax` (a letting that is a trade), `de-estimated-tax` (prepayments), `de-employee-income-tax` (employees who must file), `de-non-resident-tax` (non-resident landlords). | [31] / ['- **Related skills.** `de-capital-gains` (sales), `germany-vat-return` and `de-vat-annual-and-special-schemes` (VAT), `de-trade-tax` (a letting that is a trade), `de-estimated-tax` (prepayments), `de-employee-income-tax` (employees who must file), `de-non-resident-tax` (non-resident landlords).'] |

| main62b7639a baseline / 67 | - **Income**: rent; Umlagen (running costs paid by the tenant); settlement back-payments; a deposit kept and set off against rent, running costs or damage; payments for antenna, wind or Wi-Fi sites; a furniture surcharge; VAT received on an opted let; grants for rent (2025 instructions, lines 13 to 31). A refundable deposit is not income while held. | [67] / ['- **Income**: rent; Umlagen (running costs paid by the tenant); settlement back-payments; a deposit kept and set off against rent, running costs or damage; payments for antenna, wind or Wi-Fi sites; a furniture surcharge; VAT received on an opted let; grants for rent (2025 instructions, lines 13 to 31). A refundable deposit is not income while held.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 68 | - **Umlagen count twice**: income when received; the running costs paid are Werbungskosten. | [68] / ['- **Umlagen count twice**: income when received; the running costs paid are Werbungskosten.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 69 | - **Cash basis** ([§ 11 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__11.html)): income when received, costs when paid. Regularly recurring items paid shortly before or after the turn of the year belong to the year they relate to. Rent received in advance for a let of more than five years **may** be spread over the period; costs paid in advance for more than five years **must** be spread. A market-level loan discount (Disagio) is deductible when paid. | [69] / ['- **Cash basis** ([§ 11 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__11.html)): income when received, costs when paid. Regularly recurring items paid shortly before or after the turn of the year belong to the year they relate to. Rent received in advance for a let of more than five years **may** be spread over the period; costs paid in advance for more than five years **must** be spread. A market-level loan discount (Disagio) is deductible when paid.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 71 | ### Werbungskosten ([§ 9 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__9.html)) | [71] / ['### Werbungskosten ([§ 9 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__9.html))'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 73 | Costs "zur Erwerbung, Sicherung und Erhaltung der Einnahmen", deductible in the year paid, only for a property that earns or is intended to earn rent. Never for a flat the client lives in or lets free. | [73] / ['Costs "zur Erwerbung, Sicherung und Erhaltung der Einnahmen", deductible in the year paid, only for a property that earns or is intended to earn rent. Costs attributable to the owner’s private use or free-use parts are not rental expenses. Letting individual rooms within the owner’s home can generate deductible rental costs; allocate those costs to the actual let part.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 75 | - **Deductible**: loan interest so far as linked to the property, and the cost of raising the loan (§ 9(1) no. 1); property tax, other public charges and building insurance (no. 2); AfA and § 7b (no. 7); liability insurance; running costs, including those passed on; Hausgeld **except** the maintenance-reserve part, which is deductible only when the owners' association spends it; management; the tax adviser's rental share; tenancy legal fees; advertising and the broker for finding a tenant; rent-account fees; maintenance. | [75] / ["- **Deductible**: loan interest so far as linked to the property, and the cost of raising the loan (§ 9(1) no. 1); property tax, other public charges and building insurance (no. 2); AfA and § 7b (no. 7); liability insurance; running costs, including those passed on; Hausgeld **except** the maintenance-reserve part, which is deductible only when the owners' association spends it; management; the tax adviser's rental share; tenancy legal fees; advertising and the broker for finding a tenant; rent-account fees; maintenance."] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 76 | - **Travel**: actual cost, or 30 cents per km **driven** by car (§ 9(1) no. 4a and § 9(3) EStG with [§ 5(2) BRKG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/brkg_2005/__5.html)). If the property were a first place of activity, the commuting allowance of EUR 0.38 per full km one way applies instead. When a let property is one is not settled on the pages read: ask the accountant. | [76] / ['- **Travel**: actual cost, or 30 cents per km **driven** by car (§ 9(1) no. 4a and § 9(3) EStG with [§ 5(2) BRKG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/brkg_2005/__5.html)). If the property were a first place of activity, the commuting allowance of EUR 0.38 per full km one way applies instead. When a let property is one is not settled on the pages read: ask the accountant.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 77 | - **Not deductible**: loan principal (Tilgung); transfer tax, notary and broker on the purchase (they go into acquisition cost); reserve payments until spent; own-use or free flats; the unpaid part of a cheap let; fines (§ 9(5) with § 4(5) no. 8 EStG); income tax, solidarity surcharge and church tax. | [77, 78, 79] / ['- **Not deductible**: loan principal (Tilgung); transfer tax, notary and broker on the purchase (they go into acquisition cost); reserve payments until spent; costs attributable to own-use or free-use parts; the unpaid part of a cheap let; fines (§ 9(5) with § 4(5) no. 8 EStG); income tax, solidarity surcharge and church tax.', '', 'For mixed private and rental use, first attribute identifiable costs directly. Allocate shared costs by a justified, documented measure, such as the actual usable-floor-area ratio, and explain a first allocation (published 2025 ELSTER instructions, applied here to the unchanged rental-expense principle, not claimed 2026 form lines). For example, an identifiable rented-room repair of EUR 1,000 plus a shared roof repair of EUR 10,000 at an independently supported 20% rental share gives EUR 3,000 rental costs before any other applicable limitation; private-only costs stay excluded. The 20% is a stipulated allocation fact, not a universal prescribed ratio.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 78 | - **§ 35c energy credit**: only for a building used **only** for the owner's own housing ([§ 35c(2) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__35c.html)), and not for costs deducted as Werbungskosten (§ 35c(3)). For a let property, energy works are maintenance or production cost under the rules below, never the § 35c credit. | [80] / ["- **§ 35c energy credit**: only for a building used **only** for the owner's own housing ([§ 35c(2) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__35c.html)), and not for costs deducted as Werbungskosten (§ 35c(3)). For a let property, energy works are maintenance or production cost under the rules below, never the § 35c credit."] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 80 | ### Building depreciation (AfA, [§ 7 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__7.html)) | [82] / ['### Building depreciation (AfA, [§ 7 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__7.html))'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 82 | - **Completion date decides the straight-line rate**, not the purchase date: 3% after 2022, 2% for 1925 to 2022, 2.5% before 1925 (§ 7(4) sentence 1 no. 2). This covers every privately held building, housing or not, including flats and separate parts of a building (§ 7(5b)). The separate 3% rate in no. 1 is for business-asset buildings not used for housing: never use it for a private shop or office completed before 2023. | [84] / ['- **Completion date decides the straight-line rate**, not the purchase date: 3% after 2022, 2% for 1925 to 2022, 2.5% before 1925 (§ 7(4) sentence 1 no. 2). This covers every privately held building, housing or not, including flats and separate parts of a building (§ 7(5b)). The separate 3% rate in no. 1 is for business-asset buildings not used for housing: never use it for a private shop or office completed before 2023.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 83 | - **Shorter actual life.** If the actual life is under 33 years (after 2022), 50 years (1925 to 2022) or 40 years (before 1925), that life may be used (§ 7(4) sentence 2). The 2025 instructions call this an exceptional case that must be proved to the tax office. Refer. | [85] / ['- **Shorter actual life.** If the actual life is under 33 years (after 2022), 50 years (1925 to 2022) or 40 years (before 1925), that life may be used (§ 7(4) sentence 2). The 2025 instructions call this an exceptional case that must be proved to the tax office. Refer.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 84 | - **Older staged rates.** A building built or bought in its completion year under an old application or contract may be on the staged rates of § 7(5). Ask what past returns used; refer any change. | [86] / ['- **Older staged rates.** A building built or bought in its completion year under an application or contract before 1 January 2006 (housing; earlier dates for the other historic branches) may be on the staged rates of § 7(5). Ask what past returns used; refer any change.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 85 | - **Acquired free of charge** (gift or inheritance): continue the previous owner's rate (2025 instructions). | [87] / ["- **Acquired free of charge** (gift or inheritance): continue the previous owner's rate (2025 instructions)."] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 86 | - **Declining-balance AfA, § 7(5a).** Instead of 3% straight-line, 5% of the remaining value ("Restwert") each year, so far as the building is used for **housing**, if all hold: it is in Germany, the EU or the EEA; the client built it, or bought it by the end of the completion year; and construction started (the date on the construction-start notice required by state law; where none is required, the client declares a voluntary notice) or the binding purchase contract was made after 30 September 2023 and before 1 October 2029. No extraordinary write-down while it runs. Switching to straight-line is allowed, then on the remaining value and remaining life. | [88] / ['- **Declining-balance AfA, § 7(5a).** Instead of 3% straight-line, 5% of the remaining value ("Restwert") each year, so far as the building is used for **housing**, if all hold: it is in Germany, the EU or the EEA; the client built it, or bought it by the end of the completion year; and construction started (the date on the construction-start notice required by state law; where none is required, the client declares a voluntary notice) or the binding purchase contract was made after 30 September 2023 and before 1 October 2029. No extraordinary write-down while it runs. Switching to straight-line is allowed, then on the remaining value and remaining life.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 87 | - **Base.** Price plus side costs (transfer tax, notary, land register, the buyer's broker) under [§ 255 HGB](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/hgb/__255.html), less the land share. Land is never depreciated. A maintenance reserve bought with a flat is excluded (2025 instructions). Grants reduce the base (line 89). A former own home let for the first time needs a first computation. | [89] / ["- **Base.** Price plus side costs (transfer tax, notary, land register, the buyer's broker) under [§ 255 HGB](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/hgb/__255.html), less the land share. Land is never depreciated. A maintenance reserve bought with a flat is excluded (2025 instructions). Grants reduce the base (line 89). A former own home let for the first time needs a first computation."] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 88 | - **Price split.** Split by the ratio of market values, not by the residual-value method (2025 instructions). The [BMF purchase-price split tool](https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/Steuern/Berechnung-Aufteilung-Grundstueckskaufpreis.html) makes or tests a split. A contract split at arm's length or an expert valuation may also be used; the tax office may challenge a high building share. | [90] / ["- **Price split.** Split by the ratio of market values, not by the residual-value method (2025 instructions). The [BMF purchase-price split tool](https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/Steuern/Berechnung-Aufteilung-Grundstueckskaufpreis.html) makes or tests a split. A contract split at arm's length or an expert valuation may also be used; the tax office may challenge a high building share."] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 89 | - **First-year month cut.** Cut one twelfth for each full month before the month of acquisition or completion (§ 7(1) sentence 4). Statute applies it to § 7(5a) (sentence 5). For straight-line AfA, the BMF letter of 21 May 2025 on § 7b says so too: "bei Fertigstellung nach dem 31. Januar wäre eine zeitanteilige Kürzung der linearen AfA im Jahr der Fertigstellung erforderlich" (example 7). Only the old staged § 7(5) rates take a full first year. | [91] / ['- **First-year month cut.** Cut one twelfth for each full month before the month of acquisition or completion (§ 7(1) sentence 4). Statute applies it to § 7(5a) (sentence 5). For straight-line AfA, the BMF letter of 21 May 2025 on § 7b says so too: "bei Fertigstellung nach dem 31. Januar wäre eine zeitanteilige Kürzung der linearen AfA im Jahr der Fertigstellung erforderlich" (example 7). Only the old staged § 7(5) rates take a full first year.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 90 | - **For example**: completed February 2026, bought with benefit and burden from 1 April 2026, building share EUR 300,000. Declining-balance: 300,000 × 5% × 9/12 = EUR 11,250 in 2026; 2027: (300,000 − 11,250) × 5% = EUR 14,437.50. Straight-line instead: 300,000 × 3% × 9/12 = EUR 6,750. | [92] / ['- **For example**: completed February 2026, bought with benefit and burden from 1 April 2026, building share EUR 300,000. Declining-balance: 300,000 × 5% × 9/12 = EUR 11,250 in 2026; 2027: (300,000 − 11,250) × 5% = EUR 14,437.50. Straight-line instead: 300,000 × 3% × 9/12 = EUR 6,750. The binding contract was made in March 2026. Total acquisition cost is EUR 400,000, including land of EUR 100,000. From April, rent is EUR 1,200 plus Umlagen of EUR 250 a month: income is 9 × (1,200 + 250) = EUR 13,050. Interest of EUR 9,000, running costs of EUR 2,250 and management of EUR 600 total EUR 11,850. The declining-balance loss is 13,050 − 11,850 − 11,250 = EUR 10,050; with straight-line AfA the loss is EUR 5,550.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 91 | - **End**: until fully written off, or the year of sale. | [93] / ['- **End**: until fully written off, or the year of sale.'] |

| main62b7639a baseline / 99 | - In the EU, or a state giving the required administrative help. If bought, bought by the end of the completion year; only the buyer claims.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 100 | - Let **for housing, against payment**, in that year and the **nine** following years. Short-term guest accommodation is not housing (§ 7b(2) no. 3).'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 101 | - New window only: the building meets "Effizienzhaus 40" with sustainability class, proven by the "Qualitätssiegel Nachhaltiges Gebäude". | [103] / ['- New window only: the building meets "Effizienzhaus 40" with sustainability class, proven by the "Qualitätssiegel Nachhaltiges Gebäude".'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 102 | - **Old window ends with 2026**: claims are allowed "letztmalig für den Veranlagungszeitraum 2026" ([§ 52(15a)](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__52.html)), even if four years have not run. A buyer who started in 2024 claims 2024 to 2026 and loses the fourth year (BMF letter, para. 16, example 1). The new window has no such end. | [104] / ['- **Old window ends with 2026**: claims are allowed "letztmalig für den Veranlagungszeitraum 2026" ([§ 52(15a)](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__52.html)), even if four years have not run. A buyer who started in 2024 claims 2024 to 2026 and loses the fourth year (BMF letter, para. 16, example 1). The new window has no such end.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 103 | - **Claw-back** of all past claims if the flat is not let for housing for ten years, is sold in that time with the gain untaxed, or later costs push it over the cliff within three years after the year of acquisition or production (§ 7b(4)). | [105] / ['- **Claw-back** of all past claims if the flat is not let for housing for ten years, is sold in that time with the gain untaxed, or later costs push it over the cliff within three years after the year of acquisition or production (§ 7b(4)).'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 104 | - **After four years**, AfA runs on the remaining value ([§ 7a(9) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__7a.html); BMF letter, para. 66). Refer the computation. | [106] / ['- **After the four-year benefit period**, regular AfA uses the remaining value and the § 7(4) percentage determined from the remaining mathematical useful life; for § 7(5a), apply its 5% rate to that remaining value ([§ 7a(9) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__7a.html); BMF letter, para. 66). The old-window 2026 sunset does not shorten that four-year period: the 2024 buyer above still uses unchanged regular AfA in 2027 and changes to § 7a(9) in 2028 (BMF para. 16). Establish the actual historical normal/special deductions and useful-life branch before computing; do not substitute a guessed partial-year balance.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 105 | - **De minimis aid**: for the new window, only claimants with farming, business or self-employed income must meet the EU de minimis rules (§ 7b(5)); the 2025 instructions treat the old window as de minimis aid and ask for the other aid received. | [107] / ['- **De minimis aid**: for the new window, only claimants with farming, business or self-employed income must meet the EU de minimis rules (§ 7b(5)); the 2025 instructions treat the old window as de minimis aid and ask for the other aid received.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 106 | - **For example**: an 80 m² flat applied for in 2024, completed and bought January 2026, Effizienzhaus 40 with the seal, let long-term, cost of the flat without land EUR 400,000 (land never enters § 7b: BMF letter, para. 43). 400,000 / 80 = EUR 5,000 per m², not over 5,200: qualifies. Base cap 80 × 4,000 = EUR 320,000; § 7b 320,000 × 5% = EUR 16,000 a year for 2026 to 2029, plus straight-line 400,000 × 3% = EUR 12,000 (January, no cut). At EUR 420,000 (EUR 5,250 per m²) there is no § 7b. | [108] / ['- **For example**: an 80 m² flat applied for in 2024, completed and bought January 2026, Effizienzhaus 40 with the seal, let long-term, cost of the flat without land EUR 400,000 (land never enters § 7b: BMF letter, para. 43). 400,000 / 80 = EUR 5,000 per m², not over 5,200: qualifies. Base cap 80 × 4,000 = EUR 320,000; § 7b 320,000 × 5% = EUR 16,000 a year for 2026 to 2029, plus straight-line 400,000 × 3% = EUR 12,000 (January, no cut). At EUR 420,000 (EUR 5,250 per m²) there is no § 7b.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 108 | Source for the letter: [BMF letter of 21 May 2025 on § 7b EStG](https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/BMF_Schreiben/Steuerarten/Einkommensteuer/2025-05-21-anwendungsschreiben-7b-estg-neu.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=5). | [112] / ['Source for the letter: [BMF letter of 21 May 2025 on § 7b EStG](https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/BMF_Schreiben/Steuerarten/Einkommensteuer/2025-05-21-anwendungsschreiben-7b-estg-neu.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=5).'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 110 | ### Maintenance or production cost | [114] / ['### Maintenance or production cost'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 112 | - **Maintenance** (Erhaltungsaufwand) keeps or restores the condition: repainting, like-for-like heating, roof repair, equivalent windows. Deductible when paid, or spread under § 82b EStDV. | [116] / ['- **Maintenance** (Erhaltungsaufwand) keeps or restores the condition: repainting, like-for-like heating, roof repair, equivalent windows. Deductible when paid, or spread under § 82b EStDV.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 113 | - **Production cost** creates something new, extends, or substantially improves beyond the original condition ([§ 255(2) HGB](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/hgb/__255.html)): balcony, attic conversion, lift, lifting the standard. The 2025 instructions: works that bring the flat or building "auf einen höheren Standard" are production or acquisition cost. Capitalised and depreciated with the building. | [117] / ['- **Production cost** creates something new, extends, or substantially improves beyond the original condition ([§ 255(2) HGB](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/hgb/__255.html)): balcony, attic conversion, lift, lifting the standard (judged mainly by heating, sanitary and electrical installations and windows). The 2025 instructions: works that bring the flat or building "auf einen höheren Standard" are production or acquisition cost. Capitalised and depreciated with the building.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 114 | - **Bought in a poor state**: making non-working essential parts work again (defective heating, water or fire damage), and change-of-use works before first use, are **acquisition** cost (2025 instructions). | [118] / ['- **Bought in a poor state**: making non-working essential parts work again (defective heating, water or fire damage), and change-of-use works before first use, are **acquisition** cost (2025 instructions).'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 115 | - **The 15% rule, acquisition-related production cost** ([§ 6(1) no. 1a EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__6.html), applied to Werbungskosten by § 9(5) sentence 2). Repair and modernisation works carried out **within three years** of acquisition become production cost if their total cost **without VAT** "15 Prozent der Anschaffungskosten des Gebäudes übersteigen". It is a cliff: over it, **all** of them are capitalised. The three years are added up, so a later job can pull earlier deductions back in. Exactly 15% is not over. Not counted: extensions, and maintenance that usually recurs every year, such as servicing. | [119] / ['- **The 15% rule, acquisition-related production cost** ([§ 6(1) no. 1a EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__6.html), applied to Werbungskosten by § 9(5) sentence 2). Repair and modernisation works carried out **within three years** of acquisition become production cost if their total cost **without VAT** "15 Prozent der Anschaffungskosten des Gebäudes übersteigen". It is a cliff: over it, **all** of them are capitalised. The three years are added up, so a later job can pull earlier deductions back in. Exactly 15% is not over. Not counted: extensions, and maintenance that usually recurs every year, such as servicing.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 116 | - **For example**: building completed 1970, bought May 2025, building share EUR 200,000, so the limit is EUR 30,000. Works without VAT: EUR 12,000 in 2025 (deducted on the 2025 return) and EUR 19,000 in 2026. Total EUR 31,000 is over EUR 30,000: all of it is production cost, including 2025, and is depreciated at 2% with the building. Refer the correction of the 2025 assessment. With 2026 works of EUR 18,000 the total is exactly EUR 30,000: not over, judge each job on its own. | [120] / ['- **For example**: building completed 1970, bought May 2025, building share EUR 200,000, so the limit is EUR 30,000. Works without VAT: EUR 12,000 in 2025 (deducted on the 2025 return) and EUR 19,000 in 2026. Total EUR 31,000 is over EUR 30,000: all of it is production cost, including 2025, and is depreciated at 2% with the building: EUR 31,000 × 2% = EUR 620 for a full year. Refer the correction of the 2025 assessment. With 2026 works of EUR 18,000 the total is exactly EUR 30,000: not over, judge each job on its own.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 117 | - **Spreading, [§ 82b EStDV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estdv_1955/__82b.html).** Larger maintenance may be spread evenly over two to five years if, **when paid**, the building is not a business asset and is **mainly** housing: housing floor area more than half the usable area; garages count as housing up to one car per flat. On sale, transfer into a business or end of letting, the rest is deducted that year. Co-owners must use the same period. Works in redevelopment areas and on listed buildings (§§ 11a, 11b EStG, §§ 7h, 7i): refer. | [121] / ['- **Spreading, [§ 82b EStDV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estdv_1955/__82b.html).** Larger maintenance may be spread evenly over two to five years if, **when paid**, the building is not a business asset and is **mainly** housing: housing floor area more than half the usable area; garages count as housing up to one car per flat. On sale, transfer into a business or end of letting, the rest is deducted that year. Co-owners must use the same period. Works in redevelopment areas and on listed buildings (§§ 11a, 11b EStG, §§ 7h, 7i): refer.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 119 | ### Furniture | [123] / ['### Furniture'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 121 | A furniture surcharge is rent. Furniture and fitted kitchens are depreciated over their useful life (§ 7(1) EStG; lines 42 to 45). An item usable on its own costing not more than EUR 800 net may be deducted at once ([§ 6(2) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__6.html) through § 9(1) no. 7). How a furniture surcharge enters the cheap-let comparison is not on an official page read: refer. | [125] / ['A furniture surcharge is rent. Furniture and fitted kitchens are depreciated over their useful life (§ 7(1) EStG; lines 42 to 45). An item usable on its own costing not more than EUR 800 net may be deducted at once ([§ 6(2) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__6.html) through § 9(1) no. 7). For the cheap-let comparison, use a furniture adjustment already provided by the local rent index without adding it twice. Otherwise add a surcharge only if it can be established from the local market for furnished lets; if no market value can be determined, no surcharge is added. Never derive it from monthly furniture AfA or a standard investment return ([BFH, 6 February 2018, IX R 14/17, paras. 18 to 20](https://www.bundesfinanzhof.de/de/entscheidung/entscheidungen-online/detail/pdf/STRE201810105?type=1646225765)).'] |

| main62b7639a baseline / 136 |   - **Period**: 30 years, counted for the cheap let from the date of its tenancy contract (para. 34, example 2).
