Earlier lets of the same flat and other flats are left out.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 137 |   - **AfA in the forecast**: the straight-line § 7(4) rate, not § 7(5a) declining balance or § 7b special depreciation actually claimed (para. 34). | [141] / ['  - **AfA in the forecast**: the straight-line § 7(4) rate, not § 7(5a) declining balance or § 7b special depreciation actually claimed (para. 34).'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 138 |   - **Estimate**: average income and costs of the last five years, unless the client shows objective changes already set in motion; future maintenance may be estimated along § 28 of the Second Computation Ordinance (II. BV). | [142] / ['  - **Estimate**: average income and costs of the last five years, unless the client shows objective changes already set in motion; future maintenance may be estimated along § 28 of the Second Computation Ordinance (II. BV).'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 139 |   - **Safety margins**: add 10% to total estimated income and deduct 10% from total estimated costs. | [143] / ['  - **Safety margins**: add 10% to total estimated income and deduct 10% from total estimated costs.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 140 |   - **Leave out** private sale gains, taxable under § 23 or not. | [144] / ['  - **Leave out** private sale gains, taxable under § 23 or not.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 141 |   - **Result**: a positive total gives full deduction of all costs; a negative total splits the let as under 50%. | [145] / ['  - **Result**: apply the published 2025 ELSTER no-loss instruction to this unchanged 2026 band: a zero or positive forecast gives full deduction; a negative forecast splits the let as under 50%. This identifies the current published administrative branch, rather than claiming that the older BMF positive-surplus wording expressly settles equality.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 142 | - **Market rent**: the local rent index (Mietspiegel); failing that, equally valid: a sworn expert's reasoned report, a rent database, or rents for at least three comparable flats (§ 558a(2) nos. 2 to 4 BGB, per the 2025 instructions). | [146] / ["- **Market rent**: the local rent index (Mietspiegel); failing that, equally valid: a sworn expert's reasoned report, a rent database, or rents for at least three comparable flats (§ 558a(2) nos. 2 to 4 BGB, per the 2025 instructions)."] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 143 | - **Outside § 21(2)**: commercial space; the 66% sentence covers only permanent home lets. A cheap let of an office to a relative: refer. | [147] / ['- **Outside § 21(2)**: commercial space; the 66% sentence covers only permanent home lets. A cheap let of an office to a relative: refer.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 145 | ### Vacancy, holiday flats and the intention to make a surplus (Liebhaberei) | [149] / ['### Vacancy, holiday flats and the intention to make a surplus (Liebhaberei)'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 147 | Source for this part: BMF letter of 8 October 2004 (BStBl I p. 933), as printed with its notes in the [official income tax handbook 2024, Anhang 30 II](https://esth.bundesfinanzministerium.de/esth/2024/C-Anhaenge/Anhang-30/II/anhang-30-II.html) ("para." below). | [151] / ['Source for this part: BMF letter of 8 October 2004 (BStBl I p. 933), as printed with its notes in the [official income tax handbook 2024, Anhang 30 II](https://esth.bundesfinanzministerium.de/esth/2024/C-Anhaenge/Anhang-30/II/anhang-30-II.html) ("para." below).'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 149 | - **Long-term home lets.** A let of a flat meant to last is taken to aim at a surplus without a test (para. 1). That presumption does not cover commercial premises or undeveloped land (note to para. 1): there the forecast below decides. | [153] / ['- **Long-term home lets.** A let of a flat meant to last is taken to aim at a surplus without a test (para. 1). That presumption does not cover commercial premises or undeveloped land (note to para. 1): there the forecast below decides.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 150 | - **Signs against the intention** (paras. 5 to 7): a let planned only for a time (a fixed-term lease with stated own use or sale, short financing, an early search for a buyer), or a sale or own use within about five years of acquisition with only losses in that time. The client can rebut by showing the decision to sell or move in came later (para. 9). | [154] / ['- **Signs against the intention** (paras. 5 to 7): a let planned only for a time (a fixed-term lease with stated own use or sale, short financing, an early search for a buyer), or a sale or own use within about five years of acquisition with only losses in that time. The client can rebut by showing the decision to sell or move in came later (para. 9).'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 151 | - **Vacancy.** Costs are deductible before the first let and during a gap once the decision to let for good is made and shown by serious, continuing efforts (agent, advertisements, a realistic rent) (paras. 25 to 27). The client carries the proof. Without such efforts, or if the flat cannot be let in the foreseeable future, the intention lapses and costs stop (notes to para. 26). | [155] / ['- **Vacancy.** Costs are deductible before the first let and during a gap once the decision to let for good is made and shown by serious, continuing efforts (agent, advertisements, a realistic rent) (paras. 25 to 27). The client carries the proof. Without such efforts, or if the flat cannot be let in the foreseeable future, the intention lapses and costs stop (notes to para. 26).'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 152 | - **Forms.** A holiday flat, or any object let **short-term** (for example through an online platform), needs **Anlage V-FeWo** as well as Anlage V (2025 instructions). | [156] / ['- **Forms.** A holiday flat, or any object let **short-term** (for example through an online platform), needs **Anlage V-FeWo** as well as Anlage V (2025 instructions). Each V-FeWo form covers up to four flats.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 153 | - **Day counts** where the owner used it, left it free to others, or could use it at any time: days of own use (including free use by others; not short stays for servicing, cleaning, renovation or checks), letting and vacancy. Own use possible at any time: split vacancy by the ratio of own use to letting. Own use limited to set times (for example let through an agent): only the reserved time is own use, and vacancy counts as letting. Never used by the owner and let only to changing guests: give the letting days usual in that place, from tourist office or municipal records. | [157] / ['- **Day counts** where the owner used it, left it free to others, or could use it at any time: days of own use (including free use by others; not short stays for servicing, cleaning, renovation or checks), letting and vacancy. Own use possible at any time: split vacancy by the ratio of own use to letting. Own use limited to set times (for example let through an agent): only the reserved time is own use, and vacancy counts as letting. Never used by the owner and let only to changing guests: give the letting days usual in that place, from tourist office or municipal records.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 154 | - **Holiday flat let only to changing guests** and otherwise held ready for them: the intention is taken as given without a test, whether the client lets it directly or through an agent (para. 16). Exception: if letting days fall short of the usual local letting time by **at least 25%**, with no obstacle to letting, a forecast is needed (note to para. 16). | [158] / ['- **Holiday flat let only to changing guests** and otherwise held ready for them: the intention is taken as given without a test, whether the client lets it directly or through an agent (para. 16). Exception: if letting days fall short of the usual local letting time by **at least 25%**, with no obstacle to letting, a forecast is needed (note to para. 16).'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 155 | - **Proving there is no own use** (para. 17): the client shows one of: letting handed to an unrelated agent with own use excluded by contract for the whole year; the flat is in or right next to the client's own home, which meets the client's needs; the client has more than one holiday flat in the place and uses only one; or letting days at least equal the local seasonal average. Otherwise the client must prove it, and with too few letting days show stronger advertising (para. 18). Short stays for servicing, key handover, cleaning, checks, repairs or owners' meetings are not own use; longer stays or stays with family need explaining (para. 19). | [159] / ["- **Proving there is no own use** (para. 17): the client shows one of: letting handed to an unrelated agent with own use excluded by contract for the whole year; the flat is in or right next to the client's own home, which meets the client's needs; the client has more than one holiday flat in the place and uses only one; or letting days at least equal the local seasonal average. Otherwise the client must prove it, and with too few letting days show stronger advertising (para. 18). Short stays for servicing, key handover, cleaning, checks, repairs or owners' meetings are not own use; longer stays or stays with family need explaining (para. 19)."] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 156 | - **Holiday flat let part of the time and used by the owner** (or reserved for own use, or lent free): the intention is always tested by forecast (para. 21), per flat, not per let (para. 34). Only costs for let days and vacancy allocated to letting go in (para. 39); mixed costs (interest, property charges, maintenance, AfA, insurance) are split by days of each use (para. 40). Vacancy: if own use is limited to set times, only those count as own use; if possible at any time, split vacancy by the ratio of own use to letting; if the extent of own use cannot be established, vacancy is split 50/50 (paras. 22, 23). The forecast then follows para. 34: 30 years, § 7(4) AfA, five-year average, 10% margins (see the cheap-let forecast above). | [160] / ['- **Holiday flat let part of the time and used by the owner** (or reserved for own use, or lent free): the intention is always tested by forecast (para. 21), per flat, not per let (para. 34). Only costs for let days and vacancy allocated to letting go in (para. 39); mixed costs (interest, property charges, maintenance, AfA, insurance) are split by days of each use (para. 40). Vacancy: if own use is limited to set times, only those count as own use; if possible at any time, split vacancy by the ratio of own use to letting; if the extent of own use cannot be established, vacancy is split 50/50 (paras. 22, 23). The forecast then follows para. 34: 30 years, § 7(4) AfA, five-year average, 10% margins (see the cheap-let forecast above).'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 157 | - **Without the intention** the losses are not recognised (Liebhaberei). If the lack of intention only shows later, final assessments can be changed under § 173(1) no. 1 AO (para. 10). | [161] / ['- **Without the intention** the losses are not recognised (Liebhaberei). If the lack of intention only shows later, final assessments can be changed under § 173(1) no. 1 AO (para. 10).'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 158 | - **Trade and VAT.** Hotel-like services may make it a trade (`de-trade-tax`). Short-term guest accommodation is not VAT-exempt ([§ 4 no. 12 sentence 2 UStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__4.html)); where VAT is due it is at 7% ([§ 12(2) no. 11 UStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__12.html)), except for services not directly part of the letting, such as breakfast, even if included in the price. Whether VAT is due turns on the small-business rule: `germany-vat-return`. No § 7b for short-term lets. | [162] / ['- **Trade and VAT.** Hotel-like services may make it a trade (`de-trade-tax`). Short-term guest accommodation is not VAT-exempt ([§ 4 no. 12 sentence 2 UStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__4.html)); where VAT is due it is at 7% ([§ 12(2) no. 11 UStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__12.html)), except for services not directly part of the letting, such as breakfast, even if included in the price. Whether VAT is due turns on the small-business rule: `germany-vat-return`. No § 7b for short-term lets.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 160 | ### VAT option on commercial lets | [164] / ['### VAT option on commercial lets'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 162 | Letting land and buildings is VAT-exempt (§ 4 no. 12 sentence 1 letter a UStG), except short-term guest rooms, parking spaces, short-term campsites and operating fixtures. The landlord may opt to tax only on a let **to another business for its business** ([§ 9(1) UStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__9.html)), and for land only so far as the tenant uses it **only** for sales that allow input VAT deduction (§ 9(2)). Never to a private tenant. Some buildings begun before 1 June 1984 or 11 November 1993 escape that test ([§ 27(2) UStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__27.html)). If opted: VAT at 19% of net rent, input VAT recoverable; on the income tax return VAT received is income (line 27) and input VAT a cost (lines 80 to 82). | [166] / ['Letting land and buildings is VAT-exempt (§ 4 no. 12 sentence 1 letter a UStG), except short-term guest rooms, parking spaces, short-term campsites and operating fixtures. The landlord may opt to tax only on a let **to another business for its business** ([§ 9(1) UStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__9.html)), and for land only so far as the tenant uses it **only** for sales that allow input VAT deduction (§ 9(2)). Never to a private tenant. Some buildings begun before 1 June 1984 or 11 November 1993 escape that test ([§ 27(2) UStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__27.html)). If opted: VAT at 19% of net rent, input VAT recoverable; on the income tax return VAT received is income (line 27) and input VAT a cost (lines 80 to 82).'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 164 | ### Losses ([§ 10d EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__10d.html)) | [168] / ['### Losses ([§ 10d EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__10d.html))'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 166 | - **Same year**: against other rental income, then other kinds of income (§ 2(3) EStG). | [170] / ['- **Same year**: against other rental income, then other kinds of income (§ 2(3) EStG).'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 167 | - **Carry-back**: up to EUR 1,000,000 (EUR 2,000,000 joint) to the year before, then the second year before; the client may waive it only in full. | [171] / ['- **Carry-back**: up to EUR 1,000,000 (EUR 2,000,000 joint) to the year before, then the second year before; the client may waive it only in full.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 168 | - **Carry-forward**: no time limit; unlimited up to total income of EUR 1 million (EUR 2 million joint), then 70% of the excess for 2024 to 2027 and 60% from 2028. Article 6 of BGBl. 2024 I No. 108 changes "70 Prozent" back to "60 Prozent" from 1 January 2028; the consolidated text on gesetze-im-internet.de does not show that yet. Fixed by a separate notice (§ 10d(4)). | [172] / ['- **Carry-forward**: no time limit; unlimited up to total income of EUR 1 million (EUR 2 million joint), then 70% of the excess for 2024 to 2027 and 60% from 2028. Article 6 of BGBl. 2024 I No. 108 changes "70 Prozent" back to "60 Prozent" from 1 January 2028; the consolidated text on gesetze-im-internet.de does not show that yet. Fixed by a separate notice (§ 10d(4)).'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 169 | - **Loss schemes**: §§ 15a and 15b apply by analogy (§ 21(1) sentence 2). Refer. | [173] / ['- **Loss schemes**: §§ 15a and 15b apply by analogy (§ 21(1) sentence 2). Refer.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 171 | ### Sale within ten years ([§ 23 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__23.html), the Spekulationsfrist) | [175] / ['### Sale within ten years ([§ 23 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__23.html), the Spekulationsfrist)'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 173 | - A sale of land or a flat is a private sale if acquisition and sale are **not more than** ten years apart. Buildings put up or extended in the period are included. | [177] / ['- A sale of land or a flat is a private sale if acquisition and sale are **not more than** ten years apart. Buildings put up or extended in the period are included.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 174 | - **Own-use exception**: used **only** for own housing between acquisition (or completion) and sale, **or** for own housing in the year of sale and the two years before. A flat let up to the sale meets neither. | [178] / ['- **Own-use exception**: used **only** for own housing between acquisition (or completion) and sale, **or** for own housing in the year of sale and the two years before. A flat let up to the sale meets neither.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 175 | - **Depreciation comes back**: the cost is **reduced** by AfA, increased depreciation and special depreciation claimed (§ 23(3) sentence 4). Gain = price less reduced cost less selling costs. | [179] / ['- **Depreciation comes back**: the cost is **reduced** by AfA, increased depreciation and special depreciation claimed (§ 23(3) sentence 4). Gain = price less reduced cost less selling costs.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 176 | - **Exemption limit**: tax-free only if all private-sale gains in the year total **less than** EUR 1,000; at EUR 1,000 or more, all is taxed. Losses offset only private-sale gains of the same year, and carry back or forward only against such gains (§ 23(3) sentences 7 and 8). | [180] / ['- **Exemption limit**: tax-free only if all private-sale gains in the year total **less than** EUR 1,000; at EUR 1,000 or more, all is taxed. Losses offset only private-sale gains of the same year, and carry back or forward only against such gains (§ 23(3) sentences 7 and 8).'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 177 | - **Gift**: a single successor who receives it free takes over the donor's acquisition date (§ 23(1) sentence 3). Heirs' communities are not named there: ask the accountant. | [181] / ["- **Gift**: a single successor who receives it free takes over the donor's acquisition date (§ 23(1) sentence 3). Heirs' communities are not named there: ask the accountant."] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 178 | - **Also**: deduct any rest of a § 82b spread in the year of sale, and check the § 7b claw-back. | [182] / ['- **Also**: deduct any rest of a § 82b spread in the year of sale, and check the § 7b claw-back.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 179 | - **For example**: bought March 2019 for EUR 300,000 (building EUR 240,000 at 2% = EUR 4,800 a year); sold at the end of February 2026 for EUR 340,000; selling costs EUR 10,000. AfA claimed: 2019, ten months = EUR 4,000; 2020 to 2025, 6 × 4,800 = EUR 28,800; 2026, two months to the sale, assumed EUR 800. Total EUR 33,600. Reduced cost EUR 266,400; gain 340,000 − 266,400 − 10,000 = EUR 63,600, all taxed. Confirm the sale-year AfA with the accountant. More in `de-capital-gains`. | [183] / ['- **For example**: bought March 2019 for EUR 300,000 (building EUR 240,000 at 2% = EUR 4,800 a year); sold at the end of February 2026 for EUR 340,000; selling costs EUR 10,000. AfA claimed: 2019, ten months = EUR 4,000; 2020 to 2025, 6 × 4,800 = EUR 28,800; 2026, two months to the sale, assumed EUR 800. Total EUR 33,600. Reduced cost EUR 266,400; gain 340,000 − 266,400 − 10,000 = EUR 63,600, all taxed. Confirm the sale-year AfA with the accountant. More in `de-capital-gains`.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 181 | ### Order of work | [185] / ['### Order of work'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 183 | Fix the building share before the three-year 15% test, which measures against it. Complete the costs before the cheap-let cut, which applies to their total. Then work out the surplus or loss per property, share it between co-owners, and apply the loss rules. | [187] / ['Fix the building share before the three-year 15% test, which measures against it. Complete the costs before the cheap-let cut, which applies to their total. Then work out the surplus or loss per property, share it between co-owners, and apply the loss rules.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 185 | **Defaults when a fact is missing.** Completion date, land share, market rent for a cheap let, or private versus business status unknown: stop and ask, because each changes the result. Unsure whether work is maintenance or production cost: treat it as production cost (capitalise) until resolved. Housing use unknown: ask, because § 7(5a), § 7b, § 21(2) and § 82b apply only to housing. | [189] / ['**Defaults when a fact is missing.** Completion date, land share, market rent for a cheap let, or private versus business status unknown: stop and ask, because each changes the result. Unsure whether work is maintenance or production cost: treat it as production cost (capitalise) until resolved. Housing use unknown: ask, because § 7(5a), § 7b, § 21(2) and § 82b apply only to housing.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 187 | ## Deadlines and filing | [191] / ['## Deadlines and filing'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 189 | - **Forms.** One Anlage V per built-on property, filed with the income tax return through ELSTER. Add Anlage V-FeWo for holiday or short-term lets, and Anlage V-Sonstige for shares in communities and funds, subletting, land without buildings and rights (2025 instructions). | [193] / ['- **Forms.** One Anlage V per built-on property, filed with the income tax return through ELSTER. Add Anlage V-FeWo for holiday or short-term lets, and Anlage V-Sonstige for shares in communities and funds, subletting, land without buildings and rights (2025 instructions).'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 190 | - **Who must file.** A landlord who is assessed anyway files Anlage V with the return. An employee whose wage tax was withheld must file if untaxed side income, such as rent, is **more than** EUR 410 ([§ 46(2) no. 1 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__46.html)); if assessed, side income of not more than EUR 410 in total is taken off again, phased out above (§ 46(3), (5)). | [194] / ['- **Who must file.** A landlord who is assessed anyway files Anlage V with the return. An employee whose wage tax was withheld must file if untaxed side income, such as rent, is **more than** EUR 410 ([§ 46(2) no. 1 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__46.html)); if assessed, side income of not more than EUR 410 in total is taken off again, phased out above (§ 46(3), (5)).'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 191 | - **2026 return** ([§ 149 AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__149.html)): without an adviser, seven months after the year: 31 July 2027 is a Saturday, so **Monday 2 August 2027** ([§ 108(3) AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__108.html)). With an adviser, the last day of February of the second following year: **Tuesday 29 February 2028**. The tax office may call for an advised return earlier (§ 149(4)). | [195] / ['- **2026 return** ([§ 149 AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__149.html)): without an adviser, seven months after the year: 31 July 2027 is a Saturday, so **Monday 2 August 2027** ([§ 108(3) AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__108.html)). With an adviser, the last day of February of the second following year: **Tuesday 29 February 2028**. The tax office may call for an advised return earlier (§ 149(4)).'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 192 | - **2025 returns being filed now.** Without an adviser: due Friday 31 July 2026, passed. With an adviser: the last day of February 2027 is a Sunday, so **Monday 1 March 2027**. The extended dates in Art. 97 § 36(3) EGAO cover only "die Besteuerungszeiträume 2020 bis 2024" ([EGAO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/aoeg_1977/art_97__36.html)). Use the 2025 Anlage V. Rules are as for 2026, except the commuting allowance, which rose for 2026 (check the 2025 rate). 2025 is the second-last year for the old § 7b window. | [196] / ['- **2025 returns being filed now.** Without an adviser: due Friday 31 July 2026, passed. With an adviser: the last day of February 2027 is a Sunday, so **Monday 1 March 2027**. The extended dates in Art. 97 § 36(3) EGAO cover only "die Besteuerungszeiträume 2020 bis 2024" ([EGAO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/aoeg_1977/art_97__36.html)). The advised 2024 return was due 30 April 2026 under that transitional rule. Use the 2025 Anlage V. Rules are as for 2026, except the commuting allowance: if a first place of activity applies, the 2025 rate was EUR 0.30 per full one-way kilometre for the first 20 km and EUR 0.38 for each further kilometre (2025 instructions); from 2026 it is EUR 0.38 from the first kilometre. 2025 is the second-last year for the old § 7b window.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 193 | - **Prepayments** ([§ 37 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__37.html)): 10 March, 10 June, 10 September, 10 December; set only if at least EUR 400 a year and EUR 100 per date. A building's rental loss reduces prepayments only for calendar years beginning **after** its acquisition or completion (§ 37(3) sentence 8); a building bought before the year it is completed counts from completion (sentence 9); the bar does not apply where § 7b is claimed (sentence 10). The same timing governs a wage tax allowance for an employee ([§ 39a(1) no. 5 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__39a.html)). For example, a landlord who bought a new flat in 2026 with a EUR 10,050 loss cannot cut her 2026 prepayments for it; she can from 2027, or at once if § 7b is claimed. | [197] / ["- **Prepayments** ([§ 37 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__37.html)): 10 March, 10 June, 10 September, 10 December; set only if at least EUR 400 a year and EUR 100 per date. A building's rental loss reduces prepayments only for calendar years beginning **after** its acquisition or completion (§ 37(3) sentence 8); a building bought before the year it is completed counts from completion (sentence 9); the bar does not apply where § 7b is claimed (sentence 10). The same timing governs a wage tax allowance for an employee ([§ 39a(1) no. 5 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__39a.html)). For example, a landlord who bought a new flat in 2026 with a EUR 10,050 loss cannot cut her 2026 prepayments for it; she can from 2027, or at once if § 7b is claimed."] |

Transfer tax (GRUNDERWERBSTEUER) goes into acquisition cost."] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 214 | ## Penalties and interest | [218] / ['## Penalties and interest'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 216 | - **Late-filing surcharge** ([§ 152 AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__152.html)). Compulsory if the return is not filed "binnen 14 Monaten nach Ablauf des Kalenderjahrs" (§ 152(2) no. 1), advised or not. For 2026 the 14 months end on 29 February 2028, the same day as the advised deadline, so for an advised return it bites from the day after that deadline. Also compulsory if a return the office called in early is not filed by the date set (no. 3). The 19-month period in no. 2 is only for farmers with a non-calendar financial year. Amount: 0.25% a month of the tax less prepayments and withheld tax, at least EUR 25 for each started month (§ 152(5)), rounded down to full euros and capped at EUR 25,000 (§ 152(10)). Not compulsory, though still possible at discretion, if the deadline was extended, the tax is nil or negative, or the tax does not exceed prepayments and withheld tax (§ 152(3)). For an unadvised 2025 return filed now the surcharge is discretionary until the 14 months run out at the end of February 2027. | [220] / ['- **Late-filing surcharge** ([§ 152 AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__152.html)). Compulsory if the return is not filed "binnen 14 Monaten nach Ablauf des Kalenderjahrs" (§ 152(2) no. 1), advised or not. For 2026 the 14 months end on 29 February 2028, the same day as the advised deadline, so for an advised return it bites from the day after that deadline. Also compulsory if a return the office called in early is not filed by the date set (no. 3). The 19-month period in no. 2 is only for farmers with a non-calendar financial year. Amount: 0.25% a month of the tax less prepayments and withheld tax, at least EUR 25 for each started month (§ 152(5)), rounded down to full euros and capped at EUR 25,000 (§ 152(10)). Not compulsory, though still possible at discretion, if the deadline was extended, the tax is nil or negative, or the tax does not exceed prepayments and withheld tax (§ 152(3)). For an unadvised 2025 return filed now the surcharge is discretionary until the 14 months run out at the end of February 2027.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 217 | - **Interest** ([§ 233a AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__233a.html)): back tax and refunds carry interest of 0.15% a month ([§ 238(1a) AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__238.html)) starting 15 months after the end of the tax year, so from 1 April 2028 for 2026. | [221] / ['- **Interest** ([§ 233a AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__233a.html)): back tax and refunds carry interest of 0.15% a month ([§ 238(1a) AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__238.html)) starting 15 months after the end of the tax year, so from 1 April 2028 for 2026.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 218 | - **§ 7b claw-back**: assessments are reopened even if final; the assessment period restarts at the end of the year of the triggering event, and § 233a(2a) AO does not apply (§ 7b(4)), so interest runs from the normal date. | [222] / ['- **§ 7b claw-back**: assessments are reopened even if final; the assessment period restarts at the end of the year of the triggering event, and § 233a(2a) AO does not apply (§ 7b(4)), so interest runs from the normal date.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 219 | - **Under-declaration** (wrong deductions, unreported rent): the tax office corrects the assessment with interest; deliberate cases are tax offences. Refer. | [223] / ['- **Under-declaration** (wrong deductions, unreported rent): refer for the appropriate return or assessment correction. Apply § 233a interest only where its conditions and statutory interest period are met. Deliberate false or incomplete tax facts that shorten tax can constitute tax offences under § 370 AO; refer.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 221 | ## Cross-border points | [225] / ['## Cross-border points'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 223 | - **Non-resident landlords** with German property are taxed on the rent ([§ 49(1) no. 6 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__49.html)). They deduct only Werbungskosten linked to the German income ([§ 50(1) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__50.html)), and their tariff is applied with the basic allowance added back unless an option applies. Compute the rental result here; liability, the § 1(3) and § 1a options, and filing are in `de-non-resident-tax`. | [227] / ['- **Non-resident landlords** with German property are taxed on the rent ([§ 49(1) no. 6 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__49.html)). They deduct only Werbungskosten linked to the German income ([§ 50(1) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__50.html)), and their tariff is applied with the basic allowance added back unless an option applies. Compute the rental result here; liability, the § 1(3) and § 1a options, and filing are in `de-non-resident-tax`.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 224 | - **German residents with property abroad**: report it; treaties usually leave rent to the state where the property is. Treaty exemption, progression and Anlage AUS: refer. | [228] / ['- **German residents with property abroad**: report the rental income and determine the treatment from the actual treaty for the property country. Treaty exemption, credit, progression and Anlage AUS: refer.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 225 | - **AfA for property elsewhere in the EU or EEA**: § 7(5a) and § 7b reach buildings in the EU or EEA (and for § 7b, states giving administrative help), which matters for a resident's foreign property only where Germany taxes it. Refer. | [229] / ["- **AfA for property elsewhere in the EU or EEA**: § 7(5a) and § 7b reach buildings in the EU or EEA (and for § 7b, states giving administrative help), which matters for a resident's foreign property only where Germany taxes it. Refer."] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 226 | - **Moving in or out during the year**: `de-partners-and-part-year`. | [230] / ['- **Moving in or out during the year**: `de-partners-and-part-year`.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 228 | ## Worked examples | [232] / ['## Worked examples'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 230 | Inputs are invented and labelled, except Example 2, which is the official example in the 2025 instructions. Each result is the rental surplus or loss before the tariff. | [234] / ['Inputs are invented and labelled, except Example 2, which is the official example in the 2025 instructions. Each result is the rental surplus or loss before the tariff.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 232 | ### Example 1: a let flat bought in 2019, full year 2026 | [236] / ['### Example 1: a let flat bought in 2019, full year 2026'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 234 | A flat completed in 1995, bought in 2019, let all of 2026 at market rent. Building share of cost EUR 180,000, so straight-line AfA at 2% (§ 7(4) no. 2(b)) is EUR 3,600. Rent EUR 950 a month plus Umlagen EUR 180. Paid in 2026: interest EUR 4,200; running costs EUR 2,160 (all passed on); Hausgeld excluding the reserve EUR 540 (the reserve part of EUR 600 is left out); a repair EUR 1,500; account fees EUR 60; principal EUR 6,000 (not deductible). Six trips by car, 300 km driven in all, at 30 cents. | [238] / ['A flat completed in 1995, bought in 2019, let all of 2026 at market rent. Building share of cost EUR 180,000, so straight-line AfA at 2% (§ 7(4) no. 2(b)) is EUR 3,600. Rent EUR 950 a month plus Umlagen EUR 180. Paid in 2026: interest EUR 4,200; running costs EUR 2,160 (all passed on); Hausgeld excluding the reserve EUR 540 (the reserve part of EUR 600 is left out); a repair EUR 1,500; account fees EUR 60; principal EUR 6,000 (not deductible). Six trips by car, 300 km driven in all, at 30 cents.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 236 | Income is (950 + 180) × 12 = EUR 13,560. Werbungskosten are 3,600 + 4,200 + 2,160 + 540 + 1,500 + 90 + 60 = EUR 12,150. The surplus of EUR 1,410 goes in line 85 and is taxed at the client's marginal rate. | [240] / ["Income is (950 + 180) × 12 = EUR 13,560. Werbungskosten are 3,600 + 4,200 + 2,160 + 540 + 1,500 + 90 + 60 = EUR 12,150. The surplus of EUR 1,410 goes in line 85 and is taxed at the client's marginal rate."] |

| main62b7639a baseline / 264 | ### Example 2: cheap let to parents, under 50% (official example, 2025 instructions) | [268] / ['### Example 2: cheap let to parents, under 50% (official example, 2025 instructions)'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 266 | A married couple own a 60 m² flat and let it to the wife's parents for EUR 270 cold rent plus EUR 150 Umlagen a month. Local market rent is 60 m² × EUR 15 = EUR 900 plus EUR 150 passable costs = EUR 1,050. Costs: AfA EUR 3,000, running costs EUR 1,800, account fees EUR 120. | [270] / ["A married couple own a 60 m² flat and let it to the wife's parents for EUR 270 cold rent plus EUR 150 Umlagen a month. Local market rent is 60 m² × EUR 15 = EUR 900 plus EUR 150 passable costs = EUR 1,050. Costs: AfA EUR 3,000, running costs EUR 1,800, account fees EUR 120."] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 268 | Rent paid including Umlagen is EUR 420, which is 40.00% of EUR 1,050: under 50%, so costs are split. The form shows income EUR 5,040 (line 32), costs in full EUR 4,920 (line 83), result before the cut EUR 120 (line 85), and the cut of 60.00% in line 87. After the cut only EUR 1,968 of costs is deductible, so the taxable surplus is EUR 3,072, EUR 1,536 for each spouse. Variants: rent of EUR 700 including Umlagen is 66.67%, fully paid; EUR 600 is 57.14%, in the forecast band: run the 30-year forecast under "Cheap lets". | [272] / ['Rent paid including Umlagen is EUR 420, which is 40.00% of EUR 1,050: under 50%, so costs are split. The form shows income EUR 5,040 (line 32), costs in full EUR 4,920 (line 83), result before the cut EUR 120 (line 85), and the cut of 60.00% in line 87. After the cut only EUR 1,968 of costs is deductible, so the taxable surplus is EUR 3,072, EUR 1,536 for each spouse. Variants: rent of EUR 700 including Umlagen is 66.67%, fully paid; EUR 600 is 57.14%, in the forecast band: run the 30-year forecast under "Cheap lets".'] |

| main62b7639a baseline / 301 | ## Ask the client first | [305] / ['## Ask the client first'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 303 | - When was the building completed, and when did you buy it (contract date, and the date benefit and burden passed)? Which AfA method and rate did past returns use? For a new flat: date of the building application, start of construction, living space in m², cost per m², energy standard. | [307] / ['- When was the building completed, and when did you buy it (contract date, and the date benefit and burden passed)? Which AfA method and rate did past returns use? For a new flat: date of the building application, start of construction, living space in m², cost per m², energy standard.'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 304 | - Is the property held privately or in a business? What share of the floor area is housing? Is any unit let short-term or as a holiday flat, and is there any own use? | [308] / ['- Is the property held privately or in a business? What share of the floor area is housing? Is any unit let short-term or as a holiday flat, and is there any own use?'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 305 | - How was the price split between land and building, and who made the split? | [309] / ['- How was the price split between land and building, and who made the split?'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 306 | - What rent and Umlagen does each tenant pay, and what is the local market rent? Is any tenant a relative? | [310] / ['- What rent and Umlagen does each tenant pay, and what is the local market rent? Is any tenant a relative?'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 307 | - What works were done since the purchase, when, and at what cost without VAT? | [311] / ['- What works were done since the purchase, when, and at what cost without VAT?'] |
| main62b7639a baseline / 308 | - Do you live in Germany? Any co-owners, loans, a sale made or planned, or wage tax withheld on a job? | [312] / ['- Do you live in Germany? Any co-owners, loans, a sale made or planned, or wage tax withheld on a job?'] |
